### [1] [Decomposing Browser Pipeline Architectures for DOM-Sourced Particle Effects: Worker Offload, WebGL, and WebAssembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23609)
*Hossein Asadi*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 31 pages, 11 figures. Data and code: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21626647

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文通过受控粒子管线分解实验，系统评估了 Web Workers、WebAssembly 和 WebGL 在不同层上的优化效果，发现局部优化未必提升端到端用户可见 FPS，并提出瓶颈感知的架构测量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 团队常将 Web Workers、WebGL 和 WebAssembly 视为可互换的性能优化手段，但它们在浏览器架构中作用于不同层，局部优化不一定带来端到端的性能提升；缺乏系统性的架构级分解测量来验证层间优化传播。

Method: 构建五个粒子管线（P1-P5）和一个非粒子 CSS 基线（P0），通过可控分解隔离线程放置、渲染器选择和模拟后端；使用可复现的测试平台测量交互节奏、高负载端到端压力和仅模拟微基准，并跨浏览器/GPU/主机（包括 Google Colab T4）复现实验。

Result: 发现四个关键结果：Worker 卸载显著提升 Chrome 交互节奏；AssemblyScript 加速模拟内核约 1.5-1.85 倍；但模拟层 WASM 优势在 25 万粒子高负载下未转化为产品 FPS 提升，且模拟仍占 CPU 预算主导；渲染器排名和性能差异随配置变化。

Conclusion: 架构优化应瓶颈感知：先识别主导层，再验证该层优化是否真正传播到用户可见 FPS，而非盲目追求局部加速。

中文摘要：局部优化并不必然在分层浏览器架构中产生端到端的优化。团队经常将 Web Workers、WebGL 和 WebAssembly 视为“使其更快”的可互换方式，然而每种手段针对的是不同的层。我们提出了一种受控的架构分解——以 DOM 源粒子管线作为具体工作负载——包括五个粒子管线（P1-P5），以及一个额外的非粒子 CSS 层基线（P0），该分解隔离了线程放置、渲染器选择和模拟后端。使用可复现的测试平台，我们测量了交互节奏、高负载端到端压力、仅模拟的微基准测试，以及同主机跨浏览器/双 GPU 类别的复现（Firefox 153+Intel UHD；Chrome 138+NVIDIA NVK），并在 Google Colab（Chrome 150，Tesla T4，n=5）上进行独立的第二主机切片。四个结果突出显示：(1) Worker 卸载在论文主用 Chrome 上改善了交互节奏（约 144 对约 52 FPS；Cliff's delta=1，n=10）。(2) AssemblyScript 在主主机上使 Chrome 更新内核加速约 1.5-1.6 倍，在 Colab T4 上约 1.85 倍（仅模拟）。(3) 在约 25 万个 WebGL 粒子下，Chrome 仅模拟的 WASM 优势不一定会提高产品 FPS（主主机/Firefox 上 P5<=P4；Colab T4 上 P5≈P4）；工作线程内 CPU 阶段计时器显示模拟仍然占主导地位，因此非转换并非简单的“绘制主导 CPU”的故事。(4) 渲染器排名和绝对差异因浏览器/GPU/主机配置而异。贡献是瓶颈感知的架构测量：识别主导层并测试层优势是否传播到用户可见的 FPS。

Original abstract: Local optimization does not necessarily yield end-to-end optimization in layered browser architectures. Teams often treat Web Workers, WebGL, and WebAssembly as interchangeable ways to "make it faster," yet each lever targets a different layer. We present a controlled architectural decomposition--using DOM-sourced particle pipelines as a concrete workload--of five particle pipelines (P1-P5), with an additional non-particle CSS-layer baseline (P0), that isolates thread placement, renderer choice, and simulation backend. Using a reproducible harness we measure interactive pacing, high-load end-to-end stress, and a simulation-only microbenchmark, plus same-host cross-browser / dual-GPU-class replication (Firefox 153+Intel UHD; Chrome 138+NVIDIA NVK) and an independent second-host slice on Google Colab (Chrome 150, Tesla T4, n=5). Four results stand out. (1) Worker offload improves interactive pacing on paper-primary Chrome (approx. 144 vs approx. 52 FPS; Cliff's delta=1, n=10). (2) AssemblyScript speeds the Chrome update kernel by about 1.5-1.6x on the primary host and approx. 1.85x on Colab T4 (sim-only). (3) Under approx. 250k WebGL particles the Chrome sim-only WASM win need not raise product FPS (P5<=P4 on primary/Firefox; P5 approx. P4 on Colab T4); in-worker CPU phase timers show simulate still dominates the accounted budget, so the non-translation is not a simple "draw dominates CPU" story. (4) Renderer ranking and absolute margins vary across browser/GPU/host configurations. The contribution is bottleneck-aware architectural measurement: identify the dominant layer and test whether a layer win propagates to user-visible FPS.

</details>


### [2] [From Traceability to Justifiability: Accountability Structures in Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23610)
*Rashid Azarang*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 18 pages, 2 figures. Primary-data measurement study: a 47-platform two-pass graded documentation survey and a 30-repository depth measurement; not a literature survey. Replication package: https://github.com/mentu-ai/from-traceability-to-justifiability

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: AI系统交付流水线的默认记录普遍缺乏行为元组的内容寻址身份，且采用证明工具的仓库中近半数发布仅源码版本，导致声明的绑定无法验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统从评估到部署需要可靠记录来证明被评估对象就是被部署对象，但现有流水线记录是否真能支撑这一转换声明缺乏系统评估。

Method: 对47个交付平台（20个CI/CD，27个模型服务/智能体）进行两类文档调查，采用固定三标签协议并两次评分（第二轮盲评）；同时设计工具对30个公共仓库（冻结两层框架）计算已实现的保证深度，并与声明的深度比较，基于哈希存档进行双盲评分。

Result: 无任何平台的默认记录提供行为元组的内容寻址身份（盲评0/47）；7/15采用证明工具的仓库发布仅源码版本，导致绑定无法验证；在可测量处，5/7实现了端到端绑定，两个不足均发生在身份绑定环节。

Conclusion: AI系统交付记录在结构上缺乏可证明性，而可证明性是拒绝不合理转换的关键一级；研究还明确指出了可推翻每项发现的条件，以应对记录的时效性。

中文摘要：一个促进AI系统部署的流水线发布记录，声称被评估的对象就是被部署的对象，并且证据为这种转换提供了许可。我们仅从公开材料中衡量这些记录能否表达该声明，以及在被声明之处该声明是否成立。首先，我们对47个交付平台（20个CI/CD，27个模型服务/智能体）进行了一项两类文档调查，采用一个固定的三标签协议，评分两次（第二轮盲评），每个查阅的页面都通过内容哈希和日期固定。在188个双评分的单元格中，我们发现没有任何平台的默认记录能发出行为元组（模型版本、指令、工具定义、检索和运行时配置）的内容寻址标识；盲评轮次在47个平台中对这一列的默认评分为0。与此同时，不可变的命名版本控制正成为智能体平台的默认答案（27个中有16个）：版本整数位于可变指针之后，这是工件供应链早已认为不足的一层。其次，一个工具仅从流水线发布的输出中计算已实现的保证深度，并将其与声明的深度进行比较。应用于一个冻结的两层框架，包含30个公共仓库，从哈希存档进行两次评分（第二轮盲评；单元格级别一致率分别为30个中的23和19，两轮独立发现相同的五个完整实现），最尖锐的结果是一个可验证性漏洞：为采用证明工具而选出的15个仓库中，有7个发布仅源代码的版本，因此它们工作流所声明的绑定在声明之处无法被检查。在可检查之处，大多数情况确实成立：7个可测量的采用者中有5个端到端地实现了该绑定；两个不足都落在身份绑定上。综合来看，这些结果使该领域的记录在结构上缺乏可证明性，而这正是能够拒绝转换的那一级。该调查带有到期时钟；我们说明了什么会推翻每项发现。

Original abstract: A pipeline promoting an AI system publishes records claiming the thing evaluated is the thing deployed and that the evidence licensed the transition. We measure, from public material only, whether those records can express that claim and whether it holds where declared. First, a two-class documentation survey of 47 delivery platforms (20 CI/CD, 27 model-serving/agent) under one fixed three-label protocol, graded twice (second pass blind), every consulted page pinned by content hash and date. Across 188 double-graded cells we found no platform whose default record emits a content-addressed identity of the behavioral tuple (model version, instructions, tool definitions, retrieval and runtime configuration); the blind pass grades that column default on zero of 47. Immutable nominal versioning is meanwhile arriving as the agent platforms' default answer (16 of 27): version integers behind mutable pointers, a layer the artifact supply chain already found insufficient. Second, an instrument computes realized assurance depth from a pipeline's published exhaust alone and compares it with the declared depth. Applied to a frozen two-stratum frame of 30 public repositories graded twice from a hashed archive (second pass blind; cell-level agreement 23 and 19 of 30, both passes independently finding the same five full realizations), the sharpest result is a verifiability hole: seven of the 15 repositories chosen for adopting attestation tooling publish source-only releases, so the binding their workflows declare cannot be checked where declared. Where checkable it mostly checks out: five of seven measurable adopters realize the binding end to end; both shortfalls fall at identity binding. Together the results locate the field's records structurally short of justifiability, the one rung that can refuse a transition. The survey carries an expiry clock; we state what would falsify each finding.

</details>


### [3] [REFINE: A Multi-Agent LLM Approach for Evidence-Guided Code Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23611)
*Muhammad Waseem,Aakash Ahmad,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Preprint. 450 Java files from 15 open-source systems; 1,350 model-pass outputs across three LLM configurations. The accompanying replication package will be made publicly available

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: REFINE是一个证据感知的多智能体方法，利用LLM自动生成Java文件级重构候选，在15个开源系统的450个Java文件上减少了68%以上的代码坏味道，优于直接提示基线，但输出仍需人工审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型为自动化代码重构提供了新机会，但生成的更改必须在不引入新问题或改变行为相关结构的前提下减少目标质量问题。现有方法缺乏对坏味道证据的利用和完整的验证流程，导致重构候选不可靠。

Method: REFINE结合静态分析引导的坏味道识别、坏味道感知的规划、LLM转换、自动重新分析、保持性检查和结构化报告，形成工具无关的多智能体执行流程。

Result: 在450个Java文件（15个系统）上，REFINE三种配置（GPT-5.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro、Claude Opus 4.8）分别减少68.26%、72.79%和68.49%的代码坏味道，主要坏味道减少最显著；与直接提示基线相比，中位数减少更高、编辑更小、公共方法移除更少。

Conclusion: REFINE能有效减少代码坏味道并优于基线，但更广泛的质量改进不一致，且存在残留风险（如断言更改和公共方法移除），因此输出只能作为候选，需经编译、测试、依赖分析和人工审查后再应用。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）为自动化代码重构提供了新的机会。然而，生成的更改必须减少目标质量问题，而不引入新问题或改变与行为相关的代码结构。我们引入了REFINE（具有证据感知流的集成智能体执行重构），一种工具无关的、证据感知的多智能体方法，用于生成Java文件级重构候选。REFINE结合了静态分析引导的坏味道识别、坏味道感知的规划、基于LLM的转换、自动重新分析、保持性检查和结构化报告。我们在来自15个开源系统的450个Java文件上评估REFINE，使用OpenAI GPT-5.5、Google Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview和Anthropic Claude Opus 4.8产生了1,350个模型通道输出。REFINE在三种配置中分别减少了68.26%、72.79%和68.49%的检测到的代码坏味道，其中主要坏味道的减少最为显著。一个匹配的150文件直接提示基线表明，REFINE以更小的编辑和更少的公共方法移除实现了更高的中位数代码坏味道减少。然而，更广泛的质量改进并不一致，保持性检查揭示了残余风险，包括断言/失败调用更改和公共方法移除。因此，REFINE输出应被视为重构候选，在纳入仓库或系统级设置之前需要编译、测试、依赖分析和人工审查。

Original abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new opportunities for automated code refactoring. However, generated changes must reduce targeted quality problems without introducing new issues or altering behaviour-relevant code structures. We introduce REFINE (Refactoring with Evidence-aware Flow for Integrated ageNtic Execution), a tool-agnostic, evidence-aware multi-agent approach for generating Java file-level refactoring candidates. REFINE combines static-analysis-guided smell identification, smell-informed planning, LLM-based transformation, automated re-analysis, preservation checks, and structured reporting.
  We evaluate REFINE on 450 Java files from 15 open-source systems, producing 1,350 model-pass outputs using OpenAI GPT-5.5, Google Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview, and Anthropic Claude Opus 4.8. REFINE reduces detected code smells by 68.26%, 72.79%, and 68.49% across the three configurations, respectively, with the strongest reductions observed for major smells. A matched 150-file direct-prompt baseline shows that REFINE achieves a higher median code-smell reduction with smaller edits and fewer public-method removals. However, broader quality improvements are inconsistent, and preservation checks reveal residual risks, including assert/fail-call changes and public-method removal. Therefore, REFINE outputs should be treated as refactoring candidates requiring compilation, testing, dependency analysis, and human review before adoption in repository- or system-level settings.

</details>


### [4] [SDR Driver for Precise Timing Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23614)
*Fabrizio Pollastri*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 5 pages, 3 figures, 2 tables, submitted to GRCon26 conference

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种使用AI辅助工作流为HackRF One SDR开发SoapySDR兼容驱动的方法，显著减少开发工作量并提升可维护性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本HackRF One SDR需要软件驱动来实现精确计时应用，而原有固件缺乏相关计时功能，且初始Python实现效率低下、难以维护。

Method: 扩展原始固件以支持同步采样、时间尺度控制和时钟频率调整，并利用AI辅助开发流程从现有代码库生成SoapySDR兼容驱动。

Result: 新驱动仅需有限调试即可运行，开发工作量减少一个数量级以上，且软件可维护性得到提升。

Conclusion: AI辅助开发方法能高效生成高性能驱动，为低成本SDR的精确计时应用提供了可行方案。

中文摘要：本文描述了软件驱动的开发，这些驱动使低成本HackRF One软件定义无线电（SDR）能够实现精确计时应用。原始固件被扩展了计时功能，用于同步采样、时间尺度控制和时钟频率调整。基于Python包装器的初始实现后来被重新设计为兼容SoapySDR的驱动程序。利用AI辅助开发工作流，从现有的HackRF One和SoapySDR代码库生成了新驱动程序，并且仅在有限调试后就投入运行。该方法将开发工作量减少了一个数量级以上，同时提高了软件可维护性。

Original abstract: This paper describes the development of software drivers that enable precise timing applications with the low-cost HackRF One software-defined radio (SDR). The original firmware was extended with timing functions for synchronized sampling, time-scale control, and clock-frequency adjustment. An initial implementation based on a Python wrapper was later redesigned as a SoapySDR-compatible driver. Using an AI-assisted development workflow, the new driver was generated from the existing HackRF One and SoapySDR codebases and became operational after only limited debugging. The approach reduced development effort by more than an order of magnitude while improving software maintainability.

</details>


### [5] [Rebuild Dossier: Mechanically-Enforced Specs for Agentic App Rebuilds, and What Model-Tier Failures Reveal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23616)
*Parker Fawcett*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 48 pages, 1 figure. Code and evaluation artifacts: https://github.com/Parker-Fawcett/rebuild-dossier (archived at DOI: 10.5281/zenodo.22036801)

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出rebuild-dossier工具，通过锁定接口和逐步自动化测试来改进AI代理重建流程，但实验表明其效果取决于检查机制，且存在测试可被钻空子等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明，对于强模型，简单指令胜过多智能体流程。作者希望确保重建质量，通过锁定真实接口和强制测试来改进，但需要评估这一方法是否真正有效。

Method: 工具在编写代码前锁定应用的确切输入/输出，通过自动化检查而非书面指令强制一次一个测试的构建。评估对比了遵守规则与违反规则的代理，以及是否优于给定源码和一条指令的基线，并采用三层验证。

Result: 遵守规则的代理在保留测试上失败而违反规则的代理全过；在小型应用上与基线打平，但在更大应用上失败且自动化检查未运行；三层检查捕获了真实错误。风险在更强模型上重现，但较弱模型从未成功遵循流程。

Conclusion: 该工具公开且采用MIT许可，可端到端复现。主要结论是：通过测试并不保证正确性，自动化检查机制是关键，而非仅仅接口锁定。

中文摘要：一个AI代理的重建效果取决于生成它的流程。先前的工作发现，一旦模型足够强大，多智能体重建流程就会输给最简单的方法：将原始代码和一条指令提供给模型（AgentModernize）。我们提出了rebuild-dossier，一个开源工具，它在编写任何代码之前锁定应用程序的真实接口——即其精确的输入和输出——然后通过自动化检查（而非仅凭书面指令）强制一次一个测试的构建。三项结果构成了本次评估，其证据充分程度各不相同。首先，在一项小型对比中，遵守规则的代理未能通过一个保留测试，而违反规则的代理则全部通过——这证明当测试可以被钻空子时，通过测试套件并不能证明正确性。其次，我们测试了这是否优于简单地将源代码和一条指令交给较弱的模型：在一个小型应用上打平，但在一个更大的应用上彻底失败，而该应用上自动化检查甚至没有运行——这表明问题在于检查机制，而非接口锁定，后者是单独成立的。第三，本文的每一项主张都在三个层面进行了检查——代理自身的报告、自动化日志和实际生成的文件——捕获了真实错误，包括我们自身日志代码中的一个bug，而单一层面检查会遗漏这些错误。这些风险在不同的模型和工具链上重现：一个更强的模型连续三次遵循了我们的流程，而较弱的模型从未做到。该工具是公开的，采用MIT许可，并可在我们自己的应用程序上进行端到端复现。

Original abstract: An AI agent's rebuild is only as good as the process that produced it. Prior work found that once a model is strong enough, a multi-agent rebuild pipeline loses to the simplest approach: giving the model the original code and one instruction (AgentModernize). We present rebuild-dossier, an open-source tool that locks an application's real interface - its exact inputs and outputs - before any code is written, then enforces one-test-at-a-time building through automated checks, not written instructions alone.
  Three results shape this evaluation, with differing amounts of evidence. First, in a small comparison, the compliant agent failed a held-back test while the rule-breaking agent passed everything - proof that a passing suite doesn't certify correctness when tests can be gamed. Second, we tested whether this beats simply giving the weaker model the source and one instruction: tied on a small app, but lost outright on a larger one where the automated check wasn't even running - pointing to the check mechanism, not interface-locking, which held up separately. Third, every claim here is checked at three levels - the agent's own report, an automated log, and the actual files produced - catching real errors, including a bug in our own logging code, that a single level would have missed.
  These risks reproduce on a different model and toolchain: a stronger model followed our process three times running, something the weaker model never managed. The tool is public, MIT licensed, and reproduces end to end against our own applications.

</details>


### [6] [Identifying Latent Declarative Representations of Code for Assisting Repository Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23619)
*Shraddha Surana,Ashwin Srinivasan,Michael Bain*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 18 pages

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出ADFD-Migrate，利用带注释的数据流图作为中间表示，通过LLM引导仓库级代码迁移，在50个Fortran仓库上显著提升移植成功率和完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留代码缺乏文档，直接迁移难以处理仓库级规模；作者假设显式的语义中间表示能改善移植效果。

Method: 构建源ADFD，执行依赖感知分块生成目标代码，再与目标ADFD对比差异进行迭代修复；用静态分析覆盖检查和LLM推断。

Result: 在f2x50基准上，通过382个Probe中的327个（85.6%），40个仓库全过；所有382个行为可运行，平均迁移指数93.1%，比直接翻译高17-59个百分点。

Conclusion: 可检查的语义瓶颈（ADFD）显著提升仓库级迁移的覆盖率和集成度，并降低生成成本。

中文摘要：遗留软件仓库在无文档代码中嵌入了数十年的领域知识，使得理解和现代化改造变得困难。我们将程序视为其计算的未观察到的声明式描述的实现，并研究使这种潜在声明式表示显式化是否有助于仓库规模的移植。ADFD-Migrate通过带注释的数据流图（ADFD）来近似潜在表示，该图包含进程、数据存储、外部实体、流和行为契约。LLM在静态分析覆盖检查的引导下，从有界仓库上下文中推断源ADFD。依赖感知分块对有界进程组进行排序以进行目标语言生成。然后，源ADFD与静态恢复的目标ADFD之间的差异指导重新生成。我们在f2x50上评估了ADFD-Migrate，这是一个新的基准，包含50个Fortran仓库，代码规模从1.5k到1.6M行，分为三个复杂度层级，并从两个维度评估生成的移植结果：移植健全性（通过源神谕行为一致性来衡量）和移植完整性（通过综合迁移结果指数来衡量）。在382个精选的Fortran神谕探针中，生成的Python通过了327个（85.6%），其中40个仓库通过了所有尝试的探针。ADFD-Migrate将所有382个计划行为暴露为可运行目标，而直接翻译和仓库上下文翻译分别为99和98，静态配置文件和依赖分块消融分别为69和30。它还在47个仓库上实现了93.1%的平均迁移结果指数，以及比直接翻译高出17-59个百分点的结果指数优势。这些结果表明，可检查的语义瓶颈可以提高仓库规模迁移的覆盖率和集成度，同时为许多仓库实现更低成本的生成。

Original abstract: Legacy software repositories embed decades of domain knowledge in undocumented code, making understanding and modernization difficult. We treat a program as the implementation of an unobserved, declarative description of its computation and investigate whether making this latent declarative representation explicit improves repository-scale porting. ADFD-Migrate approximates the latent representation with an annotated data-flow diagram (ADFD) of processes, data stores, external entities, flows, and behavioral contracts. An LLM infers the source ADFD from bounded repository context, guided by static-analysis coverage checks. Dependency-aware chunking orders bounded process groups for target-language generation. Differences between the source ADFD and a statically recovered target ADFD then guide regeneration. We evaluate ADFD-Migrate on f2x50, a new benchmark of 50~Fortran repositories spanning 1.5k--1.6M lines of code and three complexity tiers, and assess the resulting ports along two dimensions: porting soundness, measured by source-oracle behavioral agreement, and porting completeness, measured by a composite migration outcome index. Against 382 curated Fortran-oracle probes, the generated Python passes 327 (85.6\%), with 40 repositories passing every attempted probe. ADFD-Migrate exposes all 382 planned behaviors as runnable targets, compared with 99 and 98 for direct and repository-context translation and 69 and 30 for the static-profile and dependency-chunking ablations. It also achieves a 93.1\% mean migration outcome index and a 17--59 percentage-point outcome-index advantage over direct translation on 47 repositories. These results suggest that an inspectable semantic bottleneck can improve the coverage and integration of repository-scale migration while enabling lower-cost generation for many repositories.

</details>


### [7] [When May an Agent Stop? Evidence-Carrying Termination for Tool-Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23623)
*Jason Liu*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出证据携带终止(ECT)：代理只有在类型化证书将答案绑定到有效轨迹证据且确定性重放验证后才可返回COMPLETE，实验显示不安全完成从252/288降至0/288，且不损害受支持完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用代理的停止决策缺乏在COMPLETE边界上的受控终止故障测试；需要在终止点上确保答案有可验证证据，防止过早或不安全的完成。

Method: 设计ECT，要求类型化证书绑定每个答案声明与范围内轨迹证据，并用确定性重放重建值；在48个合成任务、六类工具家族和八种故障上进行静态研究，再进行576轨迹预注册比较研究，与批评者核心、控制器和全轨迹LLM批评者对比。

Result: ECT在静态研究中0/288不安全完成（批评者核心252/288）；在主要保留任务簇上0/66过早终止（控制器40/66），受支持完成97/132 vs 92/132，满足非劣效性；18/66轨迹成功恢复。

Conclusion: ECT能在记录轨迹中认证支持，显著降低不安全终止且不降低完成率，但认证范围限于声明假设下的证据支持，不保证外部真值、安全性或对齐。

中文摘要：使用工具的主体必须决定何时停止。现有系统已经能够对最终成功进行门控、认证执行轨迹或强制执行运行时策略，但没有在受控终止故障的 COMPLETE 边界上测试这种特定的收据、范围和封闭重放设计。我们实例化并评估了证据携带终止（ECT）：代理只有在类型化证书将每个所需答案声明绑定到有效的、范围内的轨迹证据，并且确定性重放重建所声明的值时，才能返回 COMPLETE。一项锁定静态研究跨越六个工具使用家族中的48个完全合成任务，包含干净执行和八种故障。ECT产生0/288次不安全完成，而经过检查的终止批评者核心为252/288次（差异-87.50个百分点，95%任务簇区间[-87.50, -87.50]个百分点）。一项全新的、预指定且冻结的576轨迹研究随后将ECT与批评者核心、其忠实控制器和全轨迹LLM批评者进行比较。在22个主要保留任务簇上，ECT产生0/66次过早的不受支持的终止，而控制器为40/66次（差异-60.61个百分点，95%区间[-78.79, -40.91]个百分点），而受支持的完成率为97/132对92/132（差异3.79个百分点，区间[0.00, 9.09]个百分点），满足-10个百分点的非劣效性界限。ECT在66条轨迹中的18条中成功执行恢复，其中17条随后在有支持的情况下完成；所有三个闭环门均通过。ECT在声明的假设下认证记录轨迹中的支持，而非外部真相、安全性或对齐。

Original abstract: Tool-using agents must decide when to stop. Existing systems already gate terminal success, certify execution traces, or enforce runtime polici es, but do not test this particular receipt-, scope-, and closed-replay design at the COMPLETE boundary across controlled termination faults. W e instantiate and evaluate Evidence-Carrying Termination (ECT): an agent may return COMPLETE only when a typed certificate binds every required answer claim to valid, in-scope trace evidence and a deterministic replay reconstructs the claimed value. A locked static study crosses 48 ful ly synthetic tasks in six tool-use families with clean execution and eight faults. ECT produced 0/288 unsafe completions versus 252/288 for the inspected termination-critic core (difference -87.50 pp, 95% task-cluster interval [-87.50, -87.50] pp). A fresh, prespecified and frozen 576- trajectory study then compares ECT with the critic core, its faithful controller, and a full-trace LLM critic. On 22 primary held-out task clus ters, ECT produced 0/66 premature unsupported terminations versus 40/66 for the controller (difference -60.61 pp, 95% interval [-78.79, -40.91] pp), while supported completion was 97/132 versus 92/132 (difference 3.79 pp, interval [0.00, 9.09] pp), satisfying a -10-point noninferiority margin. ECT executed successful recovery in 18/66 trajectories, of which 17 subsequently completed with support; all three closed-loop gates p assed. ECT certifies support in a recorded trace under declared assumptions, not external truth, safety, or alignment.

</details>


### [8] [Callability Is Not Operability: Controlled Interface Interventions for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23628)
*Zihao Wang*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出代理优先工具化（AFT）和AFT-Bench框架，以弥补工具调用有效但代理无法安全继续的操作性鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准工具接口只关注调用有效性，而忽略了代理在外部效应不确定或状态模糊时所需的状态和语义信息，导致代理即使调用成功也可能无法决策。

Method: 定义工具可操作性概念，提出AFT接口机制集，并构建AFT-Bench受控实验框架，通过固定除接口外的所有因素来系统评估不同接口设计的影响。

Result: 引入了AFT-Bench，为在不同接口条件下比较代理行为提供了标准化的干预框架，但摘要中未给出具体实验结果。

Conclusion: 通过接口机制显式暴露操作相关的状态和语义（如AFT）有助于提升自主代理在操作不确定性下的安全性，且AFT-Bench可用于量化接口设计对代理操作性的影响。

中文摘要：一次工具调用可以完全有效，但仍可能让自主代理无法确定下一步该做什么。例如，如果外部效应已提交但其响应丢失，则已提交和未提交的状态可能对代理来说变得无法区分，即使它们需要不同的后续操作。我们研究了可调用性与可操作性之间的差距：工具接口是否暴露了代理在操作不确定性下安全继续所需的、与操作相关的状态和语义。我们通过“代理优先工具化”（Agent-First Tooling，AFT）来操作化工具可操作性，这是一套接口机制，涵盖选择性能力发现、执行生命周期与恢复、显式外部效应语义、机器可读结果以及后置条件验证。我们引入了 AFT-Bench，一个受控的接口干预框架，在保持任务、后端、初始状态、注入故障、代理和语言模型固定的同时，改变暴露给代理的接口。

Original abstract: A tool call can be perfectly valid yet still leave an autonomous agent unable to determine what to do next. For example, if an external effect commits but its response is lost, committed and uncommitted states may become indistinguishable to the agent even though they require different continuation actions. We study this gap between callability and operability: whether a tool interface exposes the action-relevant state and semantics needed for an agent to continue safely under operational uncertainty. We operationalize tool operability through Agent-First Tooling (AFT), a set of interface mechanisms spanning selective capability discovery, execution lifecycle and recovery, explicit external-effect semantics, machine-readable results, and postcondition verification. We introduce AFT-Bench, a controlled interface-intervention framework that holds the task, backend, initial state, injected failure, agent, and language model fixed while varying the interface exposed to the agent.

</details>


### [9] [FPGAgent: An LLM-Assisted Framework for Autonomous HLS Code Generation and Verification in FPGA Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23630)
*Tianyu Wang,Wenjie Wang,Jianguo Yao,Haibing Guan,Xijun Li*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: FPGAgent是一个多智能体框架，通过端到端可执行性验证（包括板级执行）来提升LLM生成的HLS代码的实际可用性，在HLS-Eval基准上将可综合率、可执行性和功能正确性分别平均提升16.9%、26.7%和30.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成HLS代码的方法仅验证仿真或综合，忽略了时序和布局布线约束，导致通过仿真和综合的代码仍无法在真实FPGA上运行；同时缺乏公开基准，评估规模小且自建，限制了可靠性和泛化性。

Method: FPGAgent采用多智能体框架，包含以下步骤：1) 注入HLS特定知识；2) 使用进化搜索迭代生成可靠的HLS内核实现；3) 生成C++验证程序检查功能正确性，诊断缺陷并进行针对性修复；4) 合成主机代码并在真实FPGA硬件上进行编译和板级执行，确保端到端可执行性。

Result: 在HLS-Eval基准（78个任务，跨多个领域）和真实FPGA平台上，使用五个LLM评估，FPGAgent相比现有基线平均提升可综合率16.9%、可执行性26.7%、功能正确性30.6%。

Conclusion: FPGAgent通过端到端验证显著提高了LLM生成HLS代码的实际部署能力，证明了对真实FPGA环境进行板级验证的重要性，为可执行HLS生成提供了新范式。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）在高层次综合（HLS）代码生成方面显示出巨大潜力，但大多数现有方法仅验证仿真或综合结果。由于时序和布局布线约束，*通过仿真和综合的HLS代码仍可能无法在真实FPGA平台上生成可部署、可运行的设计*。此外，缺乏公开基准导致许多评估局限于小型、自行策划的测试套件。我们提出FPGAgent，一个面向真实FPGA环境的多智能体框架，用于具有端到端可执行性验证的自主HLS编码。据我们所知，FPGAgent是*首个在成熟基准上经过实验验证的任务规格到可执行HLS生成框架*。给定自然语言任务规格，FPGAgent注入HLS特定知识，并采用进化搜索迭代推导可靠的HLS内核实现。然后生成C++验证程序以验证功能正确性，诊断潜在缺陷，并指导针对性修复。最后，合成主机代码用于在FPGA硬件上进行编译和板级执行。我们使用五个成熟的LLM在HLS-Eval（一个包含跨多个领域78个任务的基准）上全面评估FPGAgent，并在真实FPGA平台上验证板级可执行性。与现有基线相比，FPGAgent平均将可综合率提高16.9%，可执行性提高26.7%，功能正确性提高30.6%。这些结果表明FPGAgent显著提高了基于LLM的HLS生成的实际可用性，并展示了端到端验证的价值。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown substantial promise for high-level synthesis (HLS) code generation, but most existing approaches validate only simulation or synthesis results. Because of timing and place-and-route constraints, \emph{HLS code that passes simulation and synthesis may still fail to produce deployable, runnable designs on real FPGA platforms}. Moreover, the lack of public benchmarks has limited many evaluations to small, self-curated test suites. We propose FPGAgent, a multi-agent framework tailored to real FPGA environments for autonomous HLS coding with end-to-end executability validation. To the best of our knowledge, FPGAgent is \emph{the first task-specification-to-executable HLS generation framework experimentally validated on a well-established benchmark}. Given a natural-language task specification, FPGAgent injects HLS-specific knowledge and employs evolutionary search to iteratively derive reliable HLS kernel implementations. It then generates a C++ validation program to verify functional correctness, diagnoses potential defects, and guides targeted repairs. Finally, it synthesizes host code for compilation and board-level execution on FPGA hardware. We comprehensively evaluate FPGAgent with five established LLMs on HLS-Eval, a benchmark containing 78 tasks across multiple domains, and verify board-level executability on a real FPGA platform. Compared with existing baselines, FPGAgent improves the synthesizable rate by 16.9% on average, executability by 26.7%, and functional correctness by 30.6%. These results show that FPGAgent substantially improves the practical usability of LLM-based HLS generation and demonstrates the value of end-to-end validation.

</details>


### [10] [ToolRobustBench: Stage-Wise Perturbation Evaluation and Failure Diagnosis for Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23635)
*YiShan Zheng,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出ToolRobustBench，一个分阶段诊断工具调用代理鲁棒性的基准，通过工具接口、用户意图、工具输出/观察、运行时环境四类扰动，将故障归因于工具选择、模式锚定、参数绑定、反馈处理等环节。实验发现工具输出/观察扰动是主要瓶颈，混合扰动的故障模式不能由单族结果简单叠加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 干净端到端成功无法识别工具使用故障的起源和传播路径，需要阶段化诊断基准来定位问题。

Method: 构建ToolRobustBench，将四类扰动族（工具接口、用户意图、工具输出/观察、运行时环境）与工具调用流水线对齐，并将失败归因到工具选择、模式锚定、参数绑定、工具输出/运行时反馈处理和端到端任务成功。在15,456个单族实例上评估7个模型、16个本地工具、14个子类型，并进行混合族实验。

Result: 干净性能较高但不均匀，鲁棒性显著下降；工具输出/观察扰动是主要瓶颈；混合族实验显示非加性故障模式，表明单族结果不能解释组合扰动的影响。

Conclusion: ToolRobustBench提供了确定性和级联感知的基准，用于诊断超越干净工具调用准确性的鲁棒性问题，有助于理解故障传播和提升代理的可靠性。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）依赖工具调用作为基本的代理能力，使其能够调用外部系统并完成超出文本生成的任务。然而，干净端到端（E2E）的成功无法识别工具使用故障源于何处，或它如何通过一次调用传播。我们引入了ToolRobustBench，一个面向工具调用代理的分阶段诊断基准，其中工具调用代理是一个LLM系统，它选择工具、提供结构化参数并解释其返回的反馈。ToolRobustBench将四个扰动族与工具使用流程对齐：工具接口、用户意图、工具输出/观察和运行时环境扰动。它将故障归因于工具选择、模式锚定、参数绑定、工具输出/运行时反馈处理以及E2E任务成功。在7个模型、16个采样本地工具、4个扰动族和14个子类型上的15,456个单族实例实验显示，干净性能虽高但不均匀，且鲁棒性显著下降，其中工具输出/观察扰动是主要瓶颈。混合族实验揭示了无法由孤立单族结果解释的非加性故障模式。因此，ToolRobustBench提供了一个确定性和级联感知的基准，用于诊断超越干净工具调用准确性的鲁棒性；

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) rely on tool calling as a fundamental agent capability, enabling them to invoke external systems and complete tasks beyond text generation. However, clean end-to-end (E2E) success cannot identify where a tool-use failure originates or how it propagates through a call. We introduce ToolRobustBench, a stage-wise diagnostic benchmark for tool-calling agents, where a tool-calling agent is an LLM system that selects a tool, supplies structured arguments, and interprets its returned feedback. ToolRobustBench aligns four perturbation families with the tool-use pipeline: tool-interface, user-intent, tool-output/observation, and runtime-environment perturbations. It attributes failures to tool selection, schema grounding, argument binding, tool-output/runtime-feedback handling, and E2E task success. Experiments on 15,456 single-family instances across 7 models, 16 sampled local tools, 4 perturbation families, and 14 subtypes show high but non-uniform clean performance and substantial robustness degradation, with tool-output/observation perturbation the dominant bottleneck. Mixed-family experiments reveal non-additive failure patterns that are not explained by isolated single-family results. Thus, ToolRobustBench provides a deterministic and cascade-aware benchmark for diagnosing robustness beyond clean tool-calling accuracy;

</details>


### [11] [Feedback That Backfires: Why Small Language Model Agents Repeat the Call They Just Watched Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23651)
*Esmail Gumaan*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 25 pages, 6 figures, 13 tables. Code, data, probe items and rollout logs: https://github.com/Esmail-ibraheem/feedback-that-backfires

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 失败工具调用记录反而降低了模型重复该动作的概率，问题源于调用框架而非模型理解，且可行补救措施可消除大部分负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理系统假设失败调用及其错误信息有助于模型修正，但这种假设缺乏量化验证。

Method: 通过定义'修正增益'，在模拟工具调用和MBPP程序修复两个环境中，测量6个指令微调模型在记录失败调用后重新发出该动作的对数概率变化，并设计反事实与多种补救措施进行测试。

Result: 所有模型失败记录的修正增益为负，每个动作token约-1.03 nats；重复概率和贪心解码重现率显著上升。失败调用的表面形式贡献83%损害，错误消息的语义影响小且不一致。用运行时描述替换逐字调用消除76%反转，显式指令和删除失败尝试无效或更差。

Conclusion: 代理框架应当避免将失败调用的逐字文本放入上下文，转而使用运行时描述或阻止重放；同时说明对上下文污染的标准处理（删除失败尝试）适得其反。

中文摘要：代理(Agent)工具调用记录中，会记录一次失败的工具调用及其错误信息，并让模型继续，假设错误信息是修正性的信息。我们衡量它是否如此。将失败记录的定义性修正增益衡量为重新发出刚才失败的动作的对数概率的变化，我们发现对于我们在两个环境中测试的每个指令微调模型（6个检查点，135M-1.7B，4个家族）而言，增益都是负的：模拟工具调用和MBPP程序修复。按动作长度归一化后，每个动作令牌的效果约为-1.03 nats，即每个令牌的几率乘以2.8倍，并且不仅在平均值上，而且在90%-100%的单个项目上也成立。在固定的候选集上，重复失败调用的概率从0.06上升到0.54，而贪婪解码在失败后有19%的项目逐字重现，之前为0%。将同一调用与失败消息、成功消息或中性确认配对的反事实分离出两种效果：失败调用的表面形式造成83%的损害，而标记其为失败的语义贡献很小且在不同环境中符号不一致。问题出在框架上，而不是模型对错误消息的理解上，这预测了哪些补救措施有效。用运行时生成的失败描述替换逐字调用，在无令牌成本的情况下消除了76%的反转，使先前失败的字符串在解码器处不可达也作用于同一项。两种看似合理的补救措施没有效果：显式的“请勿重复”指令使测量量保持在原处，而删除失败的尝试以从干净的上下文重试——上下文污染的标准处方——是我们测量到的最糟糕的重复框架，因为它恢复了产生失败的上下文。该研究在CPU上进行端到端运行；所有工件均已发布。

Original abstract: Agent harnesses record a failed tool call and its error message in the transcript and ask the model to continue, on the assumption that the error is corrective information. We measure whether it is. Defining the corrective gain of a failure record as the change in log-probability of re-emitting the action that just failed, we find the gain is negative for every instruction-tuned model we tested (6 checkpoints, 135M-1.7B, 4 families) in two environments: simulated tool calling and MBPP program repair. Normalised by action length the effect is about -1.03 nats per action token, a factor of 2.8 in the odds of each token, and holds on 90%-100% of individual items, not only on average. Over a fixed candidate set the probability of repeating the failed call rises from 0.06 to 0.54, and greedy decoding reproduces it token for token on 19% of items after the failure versus 0% before. Counterfactuals pairing the same call with a failure message, a success message, or a neutral acknowledgement separate two effects: the failed call's surface form accounts for 83% of the damage, while the semantic contribution of marking it failed is small and inconsistent in sign across environments. The problem is in the harness, not the model's grasp of error messages, and that predicts which remedies work. Replacing the verbatim call with a runtime-generated description of the failure removes 76% of the inversion at no token cost, and making previously-failed strings unreachable at the decoder acts on the same term. Two plausible remedies do not: an explicit "do not repeat" instruction leaves the measured quantity where it was, and deleting the failed attempt to retry from a clean context, the standard prescription for context contamination, is the worst harness we measured for repetition, because it restores the context that produced the failure. The study runs end to end on a CPU; all artefacts are released.

</details>


### [12] [Beyond Executable Models: The Pufibara Agent Harness and the Modelica Agent Workflow Benchmark for Physical System Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23653)
*Zizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 针对物理系统建模中AI代理可能丢失需求或依赖过时仿真证据的问题，提出了Pufibara框架和232任务基准，在匹配LLM后端下相比Claude Code显著提高任务成功率并大幅降低资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理系统建模的AI代理面临独特挑战：模型可能编译和仿真成功但违反物理/工程要求，且代理在多次修订中可能丢失需求或使用过时候选的仿真证据。现有通用代码生成工具在此场景下表现不足，需要专门评估和框架改进。

Method: 提出Pufibara代理框架，维护跨修订的持久工程状态，将执行和仿真证据与产生它的候选版本绑定，并将提交设为显式动作；同时提出基于源代码的任务构建方法，生成包含232个任务的Modelica Agent Workflow Benchmark（模型修复、生成、调优），用代理循环外的独立评估器评分。

Result: 在DeepSeek v4 Flash后端下Pufibara通过202个任务（Claude Code为185），在Claude Sonnet 5后端下通过202个任务（Claude Code为187）；逻辑令牌总量降低76.4%-82.5%，顺序运行时间降低6.1%-58.4%。

Conclusion: 在相同LLM后端下，完整代理框架的选择对物理系统建模的任务成功率和资源使用有显著影响，Pufibara通过显式状态管理和证据绑定有效提升了性能与效率。

中文摘要：AI代理越来越多地用于仿真驱动的工程。物理系统建模与软件工程中的通用代码生成有着不同的要求，因为正确性不仅取决于语法和可执行性，还取决于物理一致性和场景相关行为。我们在Modelica（一种基于方程的建模语言）中研究这一挑战，在这种语言中，模型可能编译并仿真成功，但仍然违反其预期的物理或工程要求。在连续的版本修订中，代理可能会丢失需求跟踪，或依赖由过时候选版本产生的仿真证据。为解决这一挑战，我们提出了Pufibara，一个代理框架，它在各修订版本之间维护持久的工程状态，将执行和仿真证据与产生它的候选版本相关联，并将提交作为一种显式的代理动作。为了端到端评估Modelica代理工作流，我们还提出了一种基于源代码的、用于构建现实且可独立评估任务的方法。我们使用该方法构建了包含232个任务的Modelica代理工作流基准，涵盖模型修复、模型生成和模型调优。每个提交的候选版本都由基准拥有的、位于代理循环之外的评估器进行评分。我们将Pufibara与Claude Code作为完整框架，在两种匹配的大型语言模型（LLM）后端下进行比较。使用DeepSeek v4 Flash时，Pufibara通过了202个任务，而Claude Code通过185个。使用Claude Sonnet 5时，Pufibara通过了202个任务，而Claude Code通过187个。根据仓库报告的令牌统计，Pufibara的逻辑令牌总数降低了76.4%-82.5%。其顺序运行时间降低了6.1%-58.4%。这些发现表明，即使在匹配的LLM后端下，完整的代理框架在物理系统建模的任务成功率和资源使用方面也可能存在显著差异。

Original abstract: AI agents are increasingly used for simulation-driven engineering. Physical system modeling presents different requirements from general-purpose code generation in software engineering, because correctness depends not only on syntax and executability but also on physical consistency and scenario-dependent behavior. We study this challenge in Modelica, an equation-based modeling language in which a model may compile and simulate while still violating its intended physics or engineering requirements. Across successive revisions, an agent may lose track of requirements or rely on simulation evidence produced by an outdated candidate.
  To address this challenge, we present Pufibara, an agent harness that maintains persistent engineering state across revisions, associates execution and simulation evidence with the candidate that produced it, and makes submission an explicit agent action. To evaluate end-to-end Modelica agent workflows, we also propose a source-grounded method for constructing realistic and independently evaluable tasks. We use this method to build the 232-task Modelica Agent Workflow Benchmark, spanning Model Repair, Model Generation, and Model Tuning. Each submitted candidate is scored by a benchmark-owned evaluator outside the agent loop.
  We compare Pufibara with Claude Code as complete harnesses under two matched large language model (LLM) backends. With DeepSeek v4 Flash, Pufibara passes 202 tasks, compared with 185 for Claude Code. With Claude Sonnet 5, Pufibara passes 202 tasks, compared with 187 for Claude Code. Under the repository-reported token accounting, Pufibara records 76.4%-82.5% lower logical-token totals. Its sequential runtime is 6.1%-58.4% lower. These findings show that, even under matched LLM backends, complete agent harnesses can differ substantially in both task success and resource use for physical system modeling.

</details>


### [13] [Confidently Wrong, Silently So: Auditing Undetectable Failures of a Deployed On-Device Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23663)
*Shashwat Pandey,Satwik Pandey,Suresh Raghu*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 8 pages, 5 figures, 2 tables

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 对开发者可访问的端侧模型进行可靠性审计，发现其校准存在任务不对称问题，且幻觉与拒答在表面上不可区分，但一致性包装器能显著提升可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧模型广泛部署但缺乏服务端审核，开发者实际配置很少被独立审计，用户和资源受限的开发者难以判断模型何时出错，因此需要可重现的可靠性审计来回答这一监督问题。

Method: 采用红队测试方法，从校准、错误前提问题（幻觉）和良性提示过度拒答三个维度审计模型；训练基于15个用户可见特征的分类器测试表面不可区分性；并评估单次生成信号及黑盒一致性包装器的有效性。

Result: 发现任务不对称的校准错误：69%错误前提产生幻觉、18%良性输入被拒；置信度饱和且无区分度（AUROC 0.47，ECE 70）；表面不可区分性分类器AUROC仅0.55；单次生成信号最高AUROC≤0.68；一致性包装器将自信幻觉从75%降至3%，选择性准确率从43%提升至83%。

Conclusion: 该审计提供模型无关的协议和表面不可区分性测试，证明无需模型访问的一致性包装器可恢复可靠性，并开源代码与评估项，为审计已部署模型提供可复用基础设施。

中文摘要：对齐已部署的语言模型需要知道其输出何时可以被信任，然而如今端侧模型已随数亿台设备一同发布，却没有服务端审核，且开发者实际能够部署的配置很少被独立审计。我们针对开发者可访问的端侧基础模型开展了一项可重现的可靠性审计，并将其框定为一个监督问题：用户或资源受限的开发者能否判断模型何时出错？通过对校准、错误前提问题上的自信幻觉以及对良性提示的过度拒答进行红队测试，我们发现了一种“任务不对称的校准错误”：其护栏在不同任务上以相反方向失效（在69%的错误前提上产生幻觉，同时对18%的完全良性输入进行拒答），而其自我报告的置信度饱和且缺乏区分度（AUROC为0.47；ECE为70，在可比的小型模型中表现最差）。关键在于，自信正确和自信错误的输出在表面上是不可区分的：一个基于15个用户可见特征的分类器将其分开的AUROC仅为0.55（等价性已确认），在推理时没有留出任何监督信号。没有廉价的单次生成信号能够标记这些失败（AUROC≤0.68），而一个无需模型访问的黑盒一致性包装器能够恢复可靠性（自信幻觉从75%降至3%；选择性准确率从43%提升至83%），且成本可调。我们贡献了一个模型无关的审计协议、一个表面不可区分性测试，以及已发布的代码和冻结的评估项，作为审计已部署模型的可复用基础设施。

Original abstract: Aligning deployed language models requires knowing when their outputs can be trusted, yet on-device models now ship to hundreds of millions of devices with no server-side moderation, and the configuration developers can actually deploy is rarely audited independently. We present a reproducible reliability audit of the developer-accessible on-device foundation model, framed as an oversight question: can a user or a resource-constrained developer tell when the model is wrong? Red-teaming it on calibration, confident confabulation on false-premise questions, and over-refusal of benign prompts, we find a \emph{task-asymmetric miscalibration}: its guardrails fail in opposite directions across tasks (confabulating on 69\% of false premises while refusing 18\% of entirely benign inputs), atop a self-reported confidence that is saturated and non-discriminative (AUROC 0.47; ECE 70, worst among comparable small models). Crucially, confident-correct and confident-wrong outputs are \emph{surface-indistinguishable}: a classifier over 15 user-visible features separates them at AUROC only 0.55 (equivalence-confirmed), leaving no signal for oversight at inference time. No cheap single-generation signal flags these failures ($\le$0.68 AUROC), whereas a black-box consistency wrapper requiring no model access recovers reliability (confident confabulation 75\%$\to$3\%; selective accuracy 43\%$\to$83\%) at a tunable cost. We contribute a model-agnostic audit protocol, a surface-indistinguishability test, and released code and frozen evaluation items as reusable infrastructure for auditing deployed models.

</details>


### [14] [Enhancing Bug Report Templates in the TianoCore UEFI Firmware Development Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23754)
*Laura Baird,Neelesh Reddybattula,Nazanin Siavash,Terrance E. Boult,Armin Moin*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: ACM International Workshop on Firmware Testing and Analysis (FTA) 2026, Co-located with ISSTA 2026

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出通过增强TianoCore的GitHub Issues错误报告模板来改进错误分诊和修复，目前处于进行中，已初步获取开发者反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有错误报告模板中某些对分诊和修复有用的信息仅偶尔出现，导致效率低下，因此需要改进模板。

Method: 分析错误仓库数据，识别有用信息模式，提出在模板中增加新字段，并通过开发者访谈调整方法，后续将进行A/B测试。

Result: 目前尚无最终结果，已进行少量开发者访谈并调整了方法，计划进一步验证。

Conclusion: 通过增强模板有望提高错误分诊和解决的效率，但需更多社区反馈和验证。

中文摘要：我们建议增强GitHub Issues问题跟踪系统中TianoCore开源社区使用的错误报告模板，旨在改进错误分诊和解决过程。我们分析了错误仓库数据，发现了一些对错误分诊和修复有用的信息模式。然而，其中一些模式仅偶尔出现在错误报告的自由文本中。因此，我们建议在现有的TianoCore错误报告模板中添加几个新字段。在本研究中，我们聚焦于关键的TianoCore项目EDK II，它是各固件供应商和原始设备制造商中UEFI固件的核心。本研究目前正在进行中。到目前为止，我们已经采访了几位开发人员以获取他们的反馈并调整所提出的方法。我们计划与TianoCore社区进行更多访谈，进行A/B测试，并验证我们的方法，以实现有效且高效的错误分诊和解决。

Original abstract: We propose enhancing the bug report templates in the GitHub Issues issue tracking system used by the TianoCore open-source community with the aim of improving the bug triage and resolution process. We analyze the bug repository data and find patterns of information that are useful for bug triage and fixing. However, some of them are only occasionally included in the free-form text of bug reports. Therefore, we propose adding a few new fields to the existing TianoCore bug report template. In this study, we focus on the key TianoCore project, EDK II, which constitutes the core of the UEFI firmware across various firmware vendors and original equipment manufacturers. This study is currently a work-in-progress. So far, we have interviewed a few developers to obtain their feedback and adjust the proposed approach. We are planning more interviews with the TianoCore community to conduct A/B tests and validate our approach to achieve effective and efficient bug triage and resolution.

</details>


### [15] [SPIDER4TianoCore: Enhancing Patch-Propagation for the TianoCore UEFI Firmware Development Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23755)
*Laura Baird,Devin Haggitt,Terrance E. Boult,Aravind Machiry,Armin Moin*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: ACM International Workshop on Firmware Testing and Analysis (FTA) 2026, Co-located with ISSTA 2026

DOI: [10.1145/3842651.3843189](https://doi.org/10.1145/3842651.3843189)

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: SPIDER4TianoCore是一个Python命令行工具，用于为TianoCore/UEFI固件供应链生成集成阶段的补丁状态证据，在20个准备好的目标/CVE对上评估，能高置信度识别部分补丁前/后匹配，并保守弃权，无错误分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TianoCore/UEFI固件供应链需要集成阶段的补丁状态证据，以帮助维护者判断下游目标是否受上游补丁影响，但目前缺乏专门的工具。SPIDER的补丁传播框架提供了思路，但需要针对该供应链进行具体实现和验证。

Method: 工具接收上游补丁前后配对和准备好的下游目标，通过分析器输出四种状态（易受攻击、已修补、不适用、不确定）并附证据。在20个目标/CVE对上（来自8个EDK II仓库和2个CVE）进行评估，并与手动补丁状态标签对比。

Result: 分析器产生10个高置信度补丁前匹配和4个高置信度补丁后匹配，对6个目标保守弃权，没有产生自信的错误分类。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明SPIDER4TianoCore能为准备好的目标生成可重现的补丁状态证据，但针对一般下游目标的准确性仍需进一步研究。

中文摘要：我们提出并展示了SPIDER4TianoCore，一个打包的Python命令行工具，为TianoCore/UEFI固件供应链提供集成阶段的补丁状态证据。给定上游补丁前和补丁后的配对以及准备好的下游目标，该工具报告“易受攻击”、“已修补”、“不适用”或“不确定”，并附带供维护者审查的支持证据。我们的工作受SPIDER的补丁传播框架启发，但SPIDER4TianoCore本身并不证明补丁可以安全传播。我们在来自八个公共下游EDK II仓库和两个CVE的20个准备好的目标/CVE对上评估了该引擎。分析器产生了10个高置信度的补丁前匹配和4个高置信度的补丁后匹配，对六个目标保守地弃权，并且相对于记录的手动补丁状态标签，没有做出任何自信的错误分类。这些初步结果表明，针对准备好的目标而非一般下游准确性，可重现的证据生成是可行的。

Original abstract: We propose and demonstrate SPIDER4TianoCore, a packaged Python command-line tool that provides integration-stage patch-status evidence for the TianoCore/UEFI firmware supply chain. Given an upstream pre-patch and post-patch pair and prepared downstream targets, the tool reports Vulnerable, Already Patched, Not Applicable, or Uncertain with supporting evidence for maintainer review. Our work is inspired by SPIDER's patch-propagation framing, but SPIDER4TianoCore does not itself prove that a patch is safe to propagate. We evaluate the engine on 20 prepared target/CVE pairs from eight public downstream EDK II repositories and two CVEs. The analyzers produce 10 high-confidence pre-patch matches and four high-confidence post-patch matches, conservatively abstain on six targets, and make no confidently wrong classifications relative to the recorded manual patch-state labels. These preliminary results demonstrate reproducible evidence generation for prepared targets rather than general downstream accuracy.

</details>


### [16] [Scale, Concentration, and Entry Timing in the Shopify App Ecosystem: A Longitudinal Study of Platform Governance and Application Survival](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23771)
*Fabrizio Assabese,Peter-Jan Celis,Giuseppe Destefanis*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 基于大规模数据，Shopify应用市场高度可竞争，后来者通常增长更快，平台治理显著影响竞争格局，且应用失败可由早期数据高度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shopify应用市场具有巨大经济规模，但缺乏对其结构和采用驱动因素的实证证据，本研究旨在填补这一空白并理解平台治理的影响。

Method: 使用2025年9月全部24,826个应用的快照、7,708个应用为期366周的每周面板数据（2019年2月至2026年3月），以及通过互联网档案馆重建的上架历史，分析集中度、进入时机、平台治理变化和失败预测。

Result: 超过半数的类别为低集中度，20%为高度集中；50个类别中88%的后来者增长更快。平台自营应用进入聊天领域逆转了去集中化；Product Reviews下架释放191,000次安装，最多三分之一被竞争对手获得；2021年分成降低无显著效应。失败可预测：中位退出应用峰值安装仅8次，68周内消失，92%的类别领导权易主，前六个月数据预测两年内退出的AUC超过0.8。

Conclusion: Shopify应用市场保持可竞争性，收益分配更多取决于平台治理和进入条件，而非进入时机。

中文摘要：Shopify市场托管着超过16,000个活跃的第三方应用程序，服务270万家活跃商户店铺，估计年销售额达7060亿美元，但关于其结构和采用驱动因素的实证证据很少。我们使用2025年9月所有24,826个应用程序的快照、7,708个应用程序在366周（2019年2月至2026年3月）内的每周面板数据，以及从互联网档案馆重建的上架历史对其进行了分析。在55个已确立的功能类别中，超过一半为低集中度，20%为高度集中，且较大的类别始终集中度较低。在分析的50个类别中，88%的类别中后来者比先行者增长更快，这一现象在七年的季度重新估计中持续存在，先行者净损失检测到的安装量，而后来者则获得安装量。平台治理不对称地重塑了竞争：Shopify进入聊天领域逆转了两年多的去集中化趋势；其Product Reviews应用程序的弃用——据我们所知，这是首次测量的平台所有者退出互补者类别——释放了191,000次安装，其中最多三分之一在一年内以竞争对手采用的形式重新出现；2021年平台收入分成的降低对进入或留存没有产生可检测的变化。失败在很大程度上是无声且可预测的：退出的应用程序中位数在8次检测安装时达到峰值，并在上线后68周内从跟踪中消失；在七年内，92%的类别领导权易手；并且应用程序前六个月可公开观察的数据预测两年内退出的交叉验证AUC超过0.8。Shopify上的应用市场仍然具有可竞争性；谁受益取决于平台治理和进入条件，而非进入时机。

Original abstract: The Shopify marketplace hosts more than 16,000 active third-party applications, serving 2.7 million active merchant stores generating an estimated \$706 billion in annual sales, yet little empirical evidence exists on its structure and adoption drivers. We analyse it using a September 2025 snapshot of all 24,826 applications, a weekly panel of 7,708 applications over 366 weeks (February 2019 to March 2026), and listing histories reconstructed from the Internet Archive. Across 55 established functional categories, over half were low-concentration and 20\% highly concentrated, with larger categories consistently less concentrated. Later entrants grew faster than early movers in 88\% of the 50 analysed categories, persisting across seven years of quarterly re-estimations, with early movers on net losing detected installations whilst late movers gained them. Platform governance reshaped competition asymmetrically: Shopify's entry into chat reversed more than two years of de-concentration; the deprecation of its Product Reviews application, to our knowledge the first measured platform-owner exit from complementor category, released 191,000 installations of which at most a third reappeared as competitor adoption within a year; the 2021 reduction of the platform revenue share produced no detectable change in entry or retention. Failure is largely silent and predictable: the median exiting application peaked at 8 detected installations and disappeared from tracking within 68 weeks of launch, category leadership changed hands in 92\% of categories over seven years, and publicly observable data from an application's first six months predict exit within two years with cross-validated AUC above 0.8. App markets on Shopify remain contestable; who benefits depends on platform governance and entry conditions more than on entry timing.

</details>


### [17] [Automated Synthesis of Cloud Emulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23842)
*Archit Bhatnagar,Zhenning Yang,Sarah McClure,Yiming Qiu,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Ang Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 12 pages, 8 figures, 5 tables, Under review

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: CloudEmu提出一种利用神经符号代码合成，从云文档自动构建云模拟器的方法，其效果优于手工开发的LocalStack。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DevOps程序测试依赖真实云资源，耗时、危险且成本高；云模拟器虽可本地测试，但手动构建模拟器需解析大量文档并手工编码，难以应对云服务的复杂性和持续演化。

Method: CloudEmu结合LLM的文档理解与代码生成能力，加上云特定的符号抽象来防止幻觉确保精度，并以真实云作为预言机进行自动化测试、修复和校准。

Result: 在AWS和GCP服务上，CloudEmu在覆盖率和准确率上均表现出色，并超越了由大型团队历时十年手动开发的LocalStack。

Conclusion: 自动合成云模拟器是可行且高效的，能显著降低构建和维护成本，为云开发测试提供更可靠、可扩展的基础设施。

中文摘要：DevOps编程（例如，使用CLI/API脚本或IaC框架）是云基础设施管理的关键。与传统编程任务不同，DevOps程序测试需要针对真实云资源进行配置和执行，这通常耗时、不安全且成本高昂。云模拟器因简化DevOps程序测试而日益流行；它们通常是API级别的模拟，可以在本地环境中执行DevOps程序。然而，构建这些模拟器仍然具有挑战性：开发人员必须手动解读大量云文档，并为每个服务、API及其交互手工编写逻辑。这无法扩展到云的复杂性，而且随着服务和API的发展，云本身也是一个不断变化的目标。CloudEmu是一种自动化方法，通过神经符号代码合成，基于云文档构建模拟器。其关键思想是将LLM在文档理解和代码生成方面的通用优势与云特定的符号抽象相结合，以抑制幻觉并在规模上保证精度，同时使用真实云作为自动测试、修复和对齐的预言机。我们的评估显示了CloudEmu在主要云提供商（AWS和GCP）服务上的有效性和准确性。CloudEmu优于现有的领先工具LocalStack，后者是由一个大型工程师团队经过十余年手动开发的。

Original abstract: DevOps programming (e.g., using CLI/API scripts or IaC frameworks) is key to cloud infrastructure management. Unlike traditional programming tasks, DevOps program testing needs provisioning and execution against actual cloud resources, which is often time-consuming, unsafe, and costly. Cloud emulators have gained popularity for easing DevOps program testing; they are generally API-level mocks that can execute DevOps programs in a local environment. Still, building these emulators remains challenging: developers must manually interpret extensive cloud documentation and handcraft logic for each service, API, and their interaction. This does not scale to the complexity of the cloud, which is further a moving target as the services and APIs evolve. CloudEmu is an automated approach that constructs emulators based on cloud documentation via neurosymbolic code synthesis. The key idea is to combine LLMs' general strengths in documentation understanding and code generation with cloud-specific symbolic abstractions that suppress hallucinations and enforce precision at scale, while using the real cloud as an oracle for automated testing, repair, and alignment. Our evaluation shows the effectiveness of CloudEmu on major cloud provider (AWS and GCP) services in both coverage and accuracy. CloudEmu outperforms the existing leading tool LocalStack, which was manually developed by a large team of engineers over a decade.

</details>


### [18] [The Empire, Long Divided, Must Unite: Architectural Convergence in Three LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23953)
*Dai Jiahong*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 三个哲学相反的开源编码代理外壳在架构上收敛为五种共同元素，但均缺少外部可验证性，这预示了下一阶段的分化方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理外壳（而非底层模型）正成为代理行为的约束瓶颈，但缺乏对其架构演化的系统研究。

Method: 对三个开源编码代理外壳（deepagents、pi、dsh）进行源码级多案例研究，在固定提交点阅读代码并分析提交历史。

Result: 成熟外壳从相反方向收敛到包含五个共同元素的中间架构；第三个外壳验证了所有元素并存在直接复用；外部可验证性维度完全缺失。

Conclusion: 缺少外部可验证性不是疏忽，而是预测性缺口，将决定来源敏感领域中外壳的下一轮分化。

中文摘要：代理外壳（agent harness）是将语言模型转变为自主代理的关键：即构建模型上下文、中介其工具、运行循环并在长周期运行中持久化状态的周边代码。这一层（而非其所包裹的模型）正日益成为代理行为的约束性瓶颈。我们对三个遵循刻意对立哲学构建的开源编码代理外壳进行了源码级多案例研究：LangChain 的 deepagents（全功能自带）、Earendil 的 pi（极端极简主义）和 DeepSeek 的 dsh（一切皆插件）。在固定的提交点阅读每个外壳并追踪其提交历史后，我们发现两个成熟的外壳沿相反方向演进（deepagents 削减作者脚手架，pi 增加持久基础设施），却收敛到一种包含五个反复出现元素的架构中间形态：商品化的循环、仅追加且可重放的会话记录、模型怪癖以数据形式保留、上下文渐进式披露，以及显式的扩展接缝。事后作为留出检查阅读的第三个外壳展现出全部五个元素，并且在一个接缝中直接复用了另一个外壳的实现。因此，我们不声称独立发明，并将这种收敛分解为并行发现、扩散和字面复用。最后，一个承重维度没有收敛，甚至完全缺席：外部可验证性，即外部方无需信任运行时即可检查的防篡改记录。我们将这一缺失解读为一种预测性缺口，而非疏忽；这是面向来源敏感领域的外壳将在下一个轴向上产生分化的方向。

Original abstract: An agent harness is what turns a language model into an autonomous agent: the surrounding code that builds the model's context, mediates its tools, runs the loop, and persists state across a long-horizon run. This layer, not the model it wraps, is increasingly the binding constraint on agent behaviour. We present a source-level, multi-case study of three open coding-agent harnesses built from deliberately opposing philosophies: LangChain's deepagents (batteries-included), Earendil's pi (radical minimalism), and DeepSeek's dsh (everything-is-a-plugin). Reading each at a pinned commit and following its commit history, we find that the two mature harnesses have travelled in opposite directions (deepagents subtracting authored scaffolding, pi accreting durable infrastructure), yet converged toward one architectural middle form of five recurring elements: a commoditised loop, an append-only replayable session record, model quirks kept as data, progressive disclosure of context, and explicit extension seams. A third harness, read afterward as a held-out check, exhibits all five, and in one seam reuses another's implementation outright. We therefore do not claim independent invention, and decompose the convergence into parallel discovery, diffusion, and literal reuse. Finally, one load-bearing dimension shows no convergence, and indeed no presence: external verifiability, a tamper-evident record an outside party can check without trusting the runtime. We read this absence not as an oversight but as a predictive gap, the next axis on which harnesses for provenance-sensitive domains will differ.

</details>


### [19] [Evaluating Language Models on Cross-Language Code Functional Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23961)
*Hui Sun,Anderson Uchôa,Rohit Gheyi,Wesley K. G. Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 20 pages. To appear in the 20th International Symposium on Empirical Software Engineering and Measurement (ESEM 2026), LIPIcs vol. 394, Article No. 41

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文通过新数据集PolyHuman评估LLM在跨语言功能等价性判断上的能力，发现模型存在难度依赖的误判、对语言的敏感性、依赖表面相似性以及运行不稳定性，表明LLM并未真正掌握程序语义等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码理解评估多局限于单语言或合成代码，无法反映真实语义理解能力；需在跨语言、人类编写代码场景下验证LLM能否准确判断功能等价性。

Method: 构建PolyHuman数据集（CPP/Java/Python人类程序），评估多种LLM的语言内和跨语言等价性检测；以GPT-o4-mini为代表测试稳定性；人工分析81个系统性错误案例，分类并与Claude-Opus-4.7、Gemini-3-Flash对比。

Result: 发现等价性判断随难度增加而崩溃（更易将不等价误判为等价）；最佳模型对语言有特定敏感性（对Python更保守）；模型部分依赖相似性线索；GPT-o4-mini在相同设置下存在显著运行不稳定性。

Conclusion: 当前LLM无法可靠捕获语言内或跨语言的功能等价性，其能力表现不一致且未达到真正的语义理解水平。

中文摘要：背景：大型语言模型（LLM）在各种代码理解任务中表现出强大的性能，使得许多人相信它们能够推理程序语义。然而，现有评估主要集中于单语言设置或依赖合成生成的代码，这引发了对当前结果是否反映真实语义理解的担忧。目的：我们研究LLM是否能在人类编写的代码中准确判断跨不同编程语言的功能等价性，这种设置需要超越表面相似性的更深层次推理。方法：我们引入了PolyHuman，一个包含CPP、Java和Python人类编写程序的数据集。利用该数据集，我们评估了开放权重和专有LLM在语言内和跨语言等价性检测上的表现，并选择GPT-o4-mini作为代表性模型来评估稳定性。随后，我们人工分析了81个模型错误判断功能等价性的系统性分歧案例，检查代码逻辑和生成的思维链推理。最后，我们对这些失败进行分类，并在GPT-o4-mini、Claude-Opus-4.7和Gemini-3-Flash之间进行比较，以确定它们反映的是模型特定问题还是最先进LLM的普遍局限性。结果：我们发现了等价性判断中依赖于难度的崩溃（较难的问题使模型越来越倾向于将不等价代码误判为等价），最佳模型对编程语言的模型特定敏感性（尤其是在Python上表现更为保守），以及部分依赖于基于相似性的线索。GPT-o4-mini在相同设置下也表现出显著的运行间不稳定性，表明其能力不一致而非缺失。结论：当前的LLM无法可靠地捕获语言内或跨语言的功能等价性。

Original abstract: Background: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across a variety of code-understanding tasks, leading many to believe that they can reason about program semantics. However, existing evaluations primarily focus on single-language settings or rely on synthetically generated code, raising concerns about whether current results reflect true semantic understanding. Aims: We investigate whether LLMs can accurately judge functional equivalence across different programming languages in human-written code, a setting that requires deeper reasoning beyond superficial similarity. Method: We introduce PolyHuman, a dataset of human-written programs in CPP, Java, and Python. Using this dataset, we evaluate intra- and inter-language equivalence detection across open-weight and proprietary LLMs, selecting GPT-o4-mini as a representative model to assess stability. We then manually analyze 81 cases of systematic disagreement in which models incorrectly judge functional equivalence, examining the code logic and the generated Chain-of-Thought reasoning. Finally, we categorize these failures and compare them across GPT-o4-mini, Claude-Opus-4.7, and Gemini-3-Flash to determine whether they reflect model-specific issues or broader limitations of state-of-the-art LLMs. Results: We identify a difficulty-dependent breakdown in equivalence judgment (harder problems make the model increasingly prone to misclassifying non-equivalent code as equivalent), a model-specific sensitivity to programming language for the best-performing model (particularly a more conservative behavior on Python), and a partial reliance on similarity-based cues. GPT-o4-mini also shows substantial run-to-run instability under identical settings, indicating inconsistent rather than absent capability. Conclusions: Current LLMs do not reliably capture functional equivalence within or across languages.

</details>


### [20] [DeepRepoQA: Code Repository Question Answering with Deep Agent Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24221)
*Weihan Peng,Yuling Shi,Yingwei Ma,Longfei Yun,Beijun Shen,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出了DeepRepoQA，一个基于MCTS的代理框架，用于仓库级代码理解，在SWE-QA基准上显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库理解方法主要依赖表面级代码检索，缺乏对多文件、复杂架构和长距离代码依赖的深度推理能力。

Method: DeepRepoQA构建在代理框架上，利用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）机制让LLM代理动态搜索、导航和检查代码，实现多跳推理。

Result: 在SWE-QA基准上的综合实验表明，DeepRepoQA相比强基线取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 系统性MCTS引导的探索对于多跳仓库推理是有效的，为仓库级代码理解提供了新的方向。

中文摘要：回答开发者关于软件仓库的问题是一个关键但尚未充分探索的软件工程问题。虽然现有的仓库理解方法推动了该领域的发展，但它们主要依赖于表面级的代码检索，缺乏对多文件、复杂软件架构进行深度推理以及将答案扎根于长距离代码依赖的能力。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了DeepRepoQA，一种用于仓库级代码理解的新型问答（QA）框架。DeepRepoQA建立在代理框架之上，其中LLM代理通过对仓库结构的系统性树搜索来寻找答案。采用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）机制，使代理能够动态搜索、导航和检查代码，从而实现对长距离代码依赖的有效多跳推理。在SWE-QA基准上的综合实验表明，与强基线相比取得了显著的性能提升，验证了系统性MCTS引导探索在多跳仓库推理中的有效性。

Original abstract: Answering developer questions about a software repository is a critical yet under-explored problem in software engineering. While existing repository understanding methods have advanced the field, they predominantly rely on surface-level code retrieval and lack the ability for deep reasoning over multiple files, complex software architectures, and grounding answers in long-range code dependencies. To address these limitations, we propose DeepRepoQA, a novel question answering (QA) framework for repository-level code understanding. DeepRepoQA builds on an agentic framework where LLM agents find answers through a systematic tree search over the repository structure. A Monte-Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) mechanism is employed to empower agents to dynamically search, navigate, and inspect code, enabling effective multi-hop reasoning over long-range code dependencies. Comprehensive experiments on the SWE-QA benchmark demonstrate substantial performance gains over strong baselines, validating the effectiveness of systematic MCTS-guided exploration for multi-hop repository reasoning.

</details>


### [21] [Mutation Testing of Simulink Cyber-Physical System Models: Challenges and Solutions in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24250)
*Murat Kavak,Onur Kilincceker,Serge Demeyer,Kevin Vandenbroucke,Halim Abdurrahman Ceylan*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Accepted for publication at the 41st IEEE/ACM International Conference on Automated Software Engineering (ASE 2026), Munich, Germany, October 12-16, 2026. 7 pages

DOI: [10.1145/3832783.3834529](https://doi.org/10.1145/3832783.3834529)

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 在DUCO公司试点项目中，将变异测试应用于Simulink/StateFlow模型，发现了等价变异体、需求追踪和StateFlow变异测试等新挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变异测试可支持左移测试，尤其在缺陷成本高的网络物理系统中。DUCO公司已有模型驱动工程经验，但尚未将变异测试纳入设计模型测试，因此需要探索其应用中的挑战。

Method: 在DUCO公司开展试点项目，使用工具原型MUT4SLX对其Simulink/StateFlow模型进行变异测试，并基于经验分析遇到的问题。

Result: 发现了三个学术文献未报告的挑战：等价变异体、需求可追溯性及Stateflow变异测试的特殊困难。

Conclusion: 为在网络物理系统设计模型中使用变异测试提供了经验教训，指出了需要进一步研究的方向。

中文摘要：若干经验报告表明，变异测试能够支持左移测试策略，尤其是在后期发现缺陷会带来极高成本和风险的行业中。在网络物理系统的背景下，左移意味着工程师需要测试用于模拟、原型化和分析所设计系统可行性的设计模型。在本文中，我们分析了在将变异测试纳入DUCO（一家生产建筑通风系统的公司）过程中遇到的挑战和吸取的教训。DUCO的工程师在围绕Simulink和StateFlow的基于模型的工程方面拥有多年经验，包括对其模型的单元测试。在使用我们的工具原型MUT4SLX进行的试点项目中，我们了解到等价变异体、需求可追溯性以及StateFlow的变异测试代表了学术文献中尚未报告的特定挑战。

Original abstract: Several experience reports illustrate that mutation testing is capable of supporting a shift-left testing strategy, especially in industries where late bug discovery incurs very high costs and risks. In the context of cyber-physical systems, a shift left implies that engineers need to test the design models used to simulate, prototype, and analyse the feasibility of the system under design. In this paper, we analyse the challenges we encountered and the lessons we learned when incorporating mutation testing in the context of DUCO, a company producing ventilation systems for buildings. The engineers within DUCO have years of experience with model-based engineering centered around Simulink and StateFlow, including unit tests for their models. During a pilot project with our tool prototype MUT4SLX we learned that equivalent mutants, requirement traceability, and mutation testing for Stateflow represent particular challenges not yet reported in the academic literature.

</details>


### [22] [Cross-Stack Validation of Language-Model Training: A Clinical Fine-Tuning Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24267)
*Thang Tran,Lan Dang*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 15 pages, 3 figures, 8 tables

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轨迹级差分测试协议，用独立实现的训练栈（PyTorch 与 Zig 编写的 numbat）对整个 LoRA 微调流程进行交叉验证，发现 17 个单一实现遗漏的故障，其中数据渲染错误影响远大于数值核误差，并揭示运行时语言内存模型是一重要多样性轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络训练缺乏独立验证手段，同一技术栈下的运行无法暴露底层计算错误。先前差分测试仅针对算子和推理路径，未覆盖完整训练流程。

Method: 定义轨迹级协议：共享规范、跨算术/模型加载/数据渲染/学习轨迹的检查点、栈/编排/语言运行时独立性分离。用 PyTorch 和 numbat（Zig 框架，经 C 接口从六种语言驱动）对 Qwen3-0.6B 进行 LoRA 微调，在 168,574 个临床问答对上执行 42 次配对评估。

Result: 两个栈的保留交叉熵平均相差 0.134%，四个实现 epoch 结束时相差在 0.15% 内。发现 17 个故障，最大影响故障是临床文本渲染不匹配（损失差 0.15），远大于算术故障；四个故障仅在特定内存模型语言下可达。

Conclusion: 独立训练栈可作为全流程差分预言机，能发现传统测试遗漏的故障，且实现多样性应包括运行时和语言内存模型维度，而不仅是数值核差异。

中文摘要：神经网络训练存在一个预言机问题：一次运行可以正常收敛并产生可用模型，而其底层软件计算的却并非规定的内容。几乎所有此类工作都在同一技术栈上运行，因此很少有什么独立的东西可以对照检查。我们研究独立实现的训练栈是否可以作为整个微调流程的差分预言机，而非先前差分测试所针对的算子和推理路径。我们定义了一个轨迹级协议——共享规范、跨越算术、模型加载、数据渲染和学习轨迹的检查点，以及栈、编排和语言运行时的独立性分离——并将其应用于在 PyTorch 和 numbat（一个用 Zig 编写的独立框架，通过其 C 接口由六种语言原生驱动）下对 Qwen3-0.6B 在 168,574 个临床问答对上的 LoRA 适配。在跨越完整 epoch 的 42 次配对评估中，两个栈的保留交叉熵平均相差 0.134%，四个实现在 epoch 结束时彼此相差在 0.15% 以内。该比较暴露了 17 个单一实现开发会遗漏的故障，其中两个对软件工程而言值得注意。对训练后模型影响最大的故障位于数值核之外：临床文本渲染方式不匹配使保留损失移动了 0.15，比其旁边发现的算术故障大约 500 倍。还有四个故障仅从内存模型与前两个实现不同的语言中可达：一个跨线程迁移工作的调度器、一个对设备内存盲目的收集器、一个需要接口缺少的原语的所有权规则。实现多样性有多个轴线，运行时是其中之一。

Original abstract: Neural network training has an oracle problem: a run can converge normally and yield a usable model while the software beneath it computes something other than specified. Almost all such work runs on one stack, so there is rarely anything independent to check against. We study whether independently implemented training stacks can serve as differential oracles for a whole fine-tuning pipeline, rather than the operators and inference paths that prior differential testing targets. We define a trajectory-level protocol -- a shared specification, cross-check points spanning arithmetic, model loading, data rendering and the learning trajectory, and a separation of independence of the stack, the orchestration and the language runtime -- and apply it to a LoRA adaptation of Qwen3-0.6B over 168,574 clinical question-answer pairs under PyTorch and under numbat, an independent framework written in Zig, driven natively and through its C interface from six languages. Across 42 paired evaluations spanning a full epoch the two stacks' held-out cross-entropy differs by 0.134% on average, and four implementations end the epoch within 0.15% of one another. The comparison exposed 17 faults that single-implementation development had missed, two of them notable for software engineering. The fault with the largest effect on the trained model lay outside the numerical kernels: a mismatch in how clinical text was rendered moved held-out loss 0.15, some 500 times more than the arithmetic faults found beside it. And four faults were reachable only from a language whose memory model differs from the first two implementations: a scheduler migrating work across threads, a collector blind to device memory, an ownership discipline needing a primitive the interface lacked. Implementation diversity has several axes, and the runtime is one.

</details>


### [23] [Towards LLM-Enhanced Android Taint Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24269)
*Nicholas Miazzo,Marco Alecci,Jordan Samhi,Jacques Klein,Eleonora Losiouk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出利用大语言模型（LLM）进行智能体式交互来推理Android应用中的污点流，初步评估表明在DroidBench上显著优于FlowDroid，尤其是处理组件间通信、隐式流和反射等挑战性场景，并在真实应用中发现了额外泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态污点分析工具（如FlowDroid）在精确建模Android框架时面临挑战，导致在复杂场景下检测能力不足。作者旨在探索LLM能否有效推理污点流，以补充传统方法。

Method: 采用智能体交互策略，让LLM迭代地探索应用代码并推理数据流，从而识别污点传播路径。没有依赖静态分析模型，而是利用LLM的代码理解与推理能力。

Result: 在DroidBench上，Gemini-3 Flash的F1分数为0.96，远超FlowDroid的0.55；在组件间通信、隐式流、反射等类别上分别达到0.95、0.94、1.00，而FlowDroid仅为0.17、0.00、0.50。在真实世界应用中，还发现了FlowDroid遗漏的潜在数据泄露。

Conclusion: LLM推理能有效补充传统静态污点分析，尤其在传统方法困难的场景中表现突出，为未来构建混合LLM增强的污点分析流水线提供了方向。

中文摘要：污点分析是检测Android应用中敏感数据泄露的基本技术。然而，传统的静态工具（如FlowDroid）由于对Android框架进行精确建模的复杂性，仍然面临众所周知的挑战。在本文中，我们研究了现成的大语言模型（LLM）能否有效推理Android应用中的污点流。我们的初步方法依赖于一种智能体交互策略，使LLM能够迭代地探索代码并推理数据流。我们在DroidBench基准上对FlowDroid进行了初步评估，我们的方法优于基线：Gemini-3 Flash达到了0.96的F1分数，而FlowDroid为0.55。特别是，我们在具有挑战性的类别中观察到了改进，例如组件间通信（0.95对0.17）、隐式流（0.94对0.00）和反射（1.00对0.50），这些类别通常令FlowDroid难以处理。在一小组真实世界应用中，基于LLM的方法还识别出了FlowDroid未报告的额外潜在数据泄露。这些初步发现表明，LLM推理可能有效地补充传统的静态污点分析，激励了关于混合LLM增强污点分析管道的未来研究。

Original abstract: Taint analysis is a fundamental technique for detecting sensitive data leaks in Android apps. However, traditional static tools, such as FlowDroid, still face well-known challenges due to the complexity of accurately modeling the Android framework. In this paper, we investigate whether off-the-shelf Large Language Models (LLMs) can effectively reason about taint flows in Android apps. Our preliminary approach relies on an agentic interaction strategy, enabling the LLM to iteratively explore code and reason about data flows.
  We conduct an initial evaluation on the DroidBench benchmark against FlowDroid, where our approach outperforms the baseline: Gemini-3 Flash achieves an F1-score of 0.96, compared to 0.55 for FlowDroid. In particular, we observe improvements in challenging categories such as inter-component communication (0.95 vs. 0.17), implicit flows (0.94 vs. 0.00), and reflection (1.00 vs. 0.50), where FlowDroid typically struggles. On a small set of real-world apps, the LLM-based approach also identifies additional potential data leaks not reported by FlowDroid. These preliminary findings suggest that LLM reasoning may effectively complement traditional static taint analysis, motivating future research on hybrid LLM-enhanced taint analysis pipelines.

</details>

*Zahra Seyedghorban,Egor Klimov,Arie van Deursen,Annibale Panichella,Burcu Kulahcioglu Ozkan*

Main category: cs.SE

DOI: [10.1109/ICST69053.2026.00037](https://doi.org/10.1109/ICST69053.2026.00037)
