TL;DR: 本文介绍了 llmmas-otel，一个结合分布式追踪与故障注入的工具，用于可检查、可调试和可评估的 LLM 多智能体软件工程系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的多智能体系统在软件工程中越来越流行，但在受控故障下难以检查、调试和评估，缺乏可观测性和故障注入机制。

Method: 提出了 llmmas-otel，一个轻量级、框架无关的工具，使用 OpenTelemetry 进行分布式追踪，并在工作流阶段、智能体步骤、通信、工具调用和 LLM 调用等交互点支持定向故障注入。

Result: 在最小演示工作流和真实软件开发的 LLM 多智能体系统上进行了初步验证，证明能够可重复地比较基线和故障执行，并通过对齐轨迹和工件检查效果。

Conclusion: llmmas-otel 提供了一种有效的方式来增强 LLM 多智能体系统的可观测性和可控故障注入，有助于可重复的评估和调试。

中文摘要：基于大型语言模型的多智能体系统越来越多地被探索用于软件工程任务，但在受控故障下，它们仍然难以检查、调试和评估。我们提出了 llmmas-otel，一个轻量级且框架无关的工具，它将基于 OpenTelemetry 的分布式追踪与故障注入相结合，用于软件工程工作流中基于 LLM 的多智能体系统。该工具通过跨工作流阶段、智能体步骤、智能体间通信、工具调用和 LLM 调用的轨迹对齐遥测来检测智能体执行，并支持在选定的交互点进行有针对性的故障注入。这使得以可重复的方式比较基线和故障执行成为可能，并通过对齐的轨迹和运行工件检查其影响。我们描述了该工具在最小演示工作流和用于软件开发的真实基于 LLM 的多智能体系统上的动机、架构、实现、当前能力和初步验证。

Original abstract: Large Language Model-based multi-agent systems are increasingly explored for software engineering tasks, but they remain difficult to inspect, debug, and evaluate under controlled failures. We present llmmas-otel, a lightweight and framework-agnostic tool that combines OpenTelemetry-based distributed tracing with fault injection for LLM-based multi-agent systems in software engineering workflows. The tool instruments agent executions with trace-aligned telemetry across workflow phases, agent steps, inter-agent communication, tool calls, and LLM invocations, and supports targeted fault injection at selected interaction points. This makes it possible to compare baseline and faulty executions in a reproducible way and inspect the effects through aligned traces and run artifacts. We describe the motivation, architecture, implementation, current capabilities, and initial validation of the tool on a minimal demo workflow and a real LLM-based multi-agent system for software development.

</details>


### [25] [Ockhamareto: Pareto-Gated Segment-Level Credit Assignment for Concise Unit-Test Generation with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24473)
*Dong Huang,Mark Harman,Jie M. Zhang,Zhijiang Guo,Mingzhe Du,See Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: Ockhamareto是一个基于奥卡姆剃刀和帕累托最优的单次GRPO框架，通过帕累托门控奖励和Token级分段信用生成并选择单元测试，在多个基准上以更少测试实现更高变异得分，显著优于现有RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试生成方法往往难以同时优化测试的数量和有效性，导致生成的测试套件冗余或遗漏缺陷。需要一种能够自动权衡效率和效果的框架，并识别每个函数的关键工程权衡点。

Method: 提出Ockhamareto框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 帕累托门控奖励，仅对在(变异得分, 测试数量)空间中非支配的rollout给予奖励；2) Token级分段信用，将每个测试的边际变异杀死归因于生成该测试块的token，从而实现细粒度的信用分配。

Result: 在UnLeakedTestBench上，Ockhamareto严格帕累托支配MIST-RL，变异得分提升至49.9%（对比31.3%），测试数从4.67降至2.60，每次测试权衡改进3.4倍。在四个基准上均领先，并在4B/9B/27B模型规模上增加30-35pp变异得分。此外，帕累托前沿的膝点与函数大小等代理指标不相关。

Conclusion: Ockhamareto验证了通过帕累托最优原则和细粒度信用分配，可以在单元测试生成中同时实现更少测试和更高缺陷捕获率。帕累托前沿计算对于识别每个函数的最优权衡至关重要，而非依赖简单的代理指标。

中文摘要：我们介绍了 \textbf{Ockhamareto}，一个基于\emph{奥卡姆剃刀}和\emph{帕累托最优}原则的用于单元测试生成与选择的单次GRPO框架。Ockhamareto有两个主要组成部分：(i)~一个\emph{帕累托门控奖励}，仅奖励在（变异得分, $-$(测试数量)）空间中非支配的rollout；(ii)~\emph{Token级分段信用}，将每个测试的边际变异杀死归因于其单元测试块的token。在\emph{UnLeakedTestBench~(ULT)}上，Ockhamareto\emph{严格帕累托支配}最强的RL基线(\emph{MIST-RL})。此外，它在\emph{每一个且所有}优化目标上都占优，捕获更多缺陷（$N{=}5$时变异得分为$49.9\%$对比$31.3\%$），使用\emph{更少}的测试（平均$2.60$对比$4.67$），从而实现了$3.4\times$的每次测试权衡改进。该优势在所有四个基准测试（\emph{HumanEval+}、\emph{MBPP+}、\emph{CodeContests}、\emph{TestGenEval-Lite}）中均存在：Ockhamareto在每一个基准上同时领先变异和覆盖度量，且总是使用最小的测试套件。Ockhamareto在所有模型规模上也优于最先进水平，在4B、9B和27B模型规模上增加了$+30$--$35$~pp变异得分。我们还展示了帕累托前沿上效率与效果之间最优权衡的膝点与更易计算的明显代理指标（如函数大小）不相关。这一发现为帕累托前沿计算提供了动机；它对于识别每个被测函数的关键工程权衡是必需的。

Original abstract: We introduce \textbf{Ockhamareto}, a single-shot GRPO framework for unit-test generation and selection, based on the principles of \emph{Ockham's Razor} and \emph{Pareto Optimality}. Ockhamareto has two principal components: (i)~a \emph{Pareto-gated Bonus} that rewards only rollouts non-dominated in~(mutation, $-$\#tests) space, and (ii)~\emph{Token-level Segment Credit}, which attributes each test's marginal mutation kills back to the tokens of its unit-test block. On the \emph{UnLeakedTestBench~(ULT)}, Ockhamareto \emph{strictly Pareto-dominates} the strongest RL baseline~(\emph{MIST-RL}). Furthermore, it dominates on {\em each and all} optimization objectives, catching more bugs ($49.9\%$ vs $31.3\%$ mutation score at $N{=}5$), using \emph{fewer} tests ($2.60$ vs $4.67$ on average), thereby achieving $3.4\times$ the per-test trade-off improvement. The advantage is found in all four benchmarks~(\emph{HumanEval+}, \emph{MBPP+}, \emph{CodeContests}, \emph{TestGenEval-Lite}): Ockhamareto leads both mutation and coverage metrics on every one, always with the smallest suite. Ockhamareto also outperforms the state-of-the-art at all model scales, adding $+30$--$35$~pp mutation at 4B, 9B, and 27B model sizes. We also show that the knee point of the optimal trade-off between efficiency and effectiveness on the Pareto front is not correlated with obvious more easily computed proxy metrics, such as function size. This finding motivates the Pareto front computation; it is needed to identify this crucial engineering trade-off for each function under test.

</details>


### [26] [LumiXAI: A Modular Full-Stack Framework for Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24524)
*Alfio Ferrara,Lorenzo Gatta,Sergio Picascia,Elisabetta Rocchetti*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: LumiXAI是一个模块化全栈框架，统一了特征归因分析，结合分类与生成式归因、交互式GUI、插件架构和三层访问权限，支持可重复分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释性工具碎片化，各自只能处理单一模态、固定方法或特定用户群体，且多需要编程技能，难以供非专家使用，缺乏整合性系统。

Method: 设计LumiXAI，包含分类与生成式归因、双向探索交互GUI、插件化模型/方法注册架构，以及从同一后端为三类用户提供服务的分层访问机制，并使用容器化服务和持久化存储。

Result: 实现了一个可操作的统一系统，在同一界面、交互模型和持久化层下整合多种归因方法，支持跨机器的可重复分析。

Conclusion: LumiXAI通过单一后端和分层访问降低了使用门槛，提高了可扩展性和可重复性，是解释性工具整合的有效方案。

中文摘要：特征归因是模型可解释性的核心工具，但其应用软件仍然零散：各个工具在狭窄的维度上专门化，例如单一模态、代码API或GUI、或固定而非可扩展的方法集，很少能结合这些优势。此外，许多可解释性工具主要面向领域专家，需要编程技能或对归因方法的熟悉，这使得非专家用户难以使用。在本文中，我们提出了LumiXAI，一个模块化的全栈框架，将归因分析整合到单一系统中。它将分类和生成式归因与支持双向探索的交互式GUI相结合，提供用于注册新模型和方法的插件架构，以及从同一后端服务非程序员、开发者和扩展者三个访问层级。其贡献在于一个系统，在统一界面、统一交互模型和统一持久化层下操作成熟的归因方法，通过容器化服务和持久化结果实现跨机器的可重复分析。

Original abstract: Feature attribution is a central tool of model interpretability, yet the software through which it is applied remains fragmented: individual tools specialize along narrow axes, such as a single modality, a code API or a GUI, or a fixed rather than extensible method set, and rarely combine these strengths. Moreover, many explainability tools are designed primarily for domain experts, requiring programming skills or familiarity with attribution methods that can make them difficult for non-expert users to access. In this article, we present LumiXAI, a modular full-stack framework that consolidates attribution analysis into a single system. It couples classification and generative attribution with an interactive GUI supporting bidirectional exploration, a plug-in architecture for registering new models and methods, and three access tiers serving non-programmers, developers, and extenders from one backend. Its contribution is a system that operationalises established attribution methods under one interface, one interaction model, and one persistence layer, with containerised services and persistent results making analyses reproducible across machines.

</details>


### [27] [Quantifying the Relationship Between Team Dysfunctions and Performance in Capstone Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24634)
*Luciano Pereira Soares,Luciana Campos Lima,Rafael Corsi Ferrão*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文研究工程顶点项目中团队功能失调与表现的关系，基于Lencioni五功能失调模型，发现团队动态与表现呈弱到中等相关，强调目标一致性和交付成果的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顶点项目对评估学生职业准备至关重要，但团队动态与项目表现的关系尚不明确，需要实证研究。

Method: 使用学生团队调查数据，基于Lencioni模型量化团队动态，所有项目反向编码，分数范围3-9，分析其与表现的关系。

Result: 团队动态与表现之间存在弱到中等程度的相关性。

Conclusion: 团队动态应结合技术和情境因素考虑，尤其重视目标一致性和对交付成果的关注，为顶点项目设计提供洞见。

中文摘要：本文研究了工程顶点项目背景下团队功能失调与表现之间的关系。顶点项目被认为是评估工程和计算机科学学生专业实践准备程度的基础，不仅评估技术知识，还评估团队合作、沟通、设计和组织等基本能力。尽管团队动态在此类环境中的重要性已得到公认，但其与项目表现的关系仍未得到充分理解。利用从学生团队收集的调查数据，我们基于Lencioni的团队五种功能失调模型量化团队动态，所有测量均反向编码，使得较高的值表示更积极的团队行为（即较低的功能失调）。结果表明，团队动态与表现之间存在弱到中等程度的关系。每个功能失调得分计算为三个相应调查项目的总和。所有项目均编码为较高的值表示更积极的团队行为（即较低的功能失调）。因此，每个得分的范围从3到9，其中较高的值反映更健康的团队动态。本研究分析了项目式学习环境中团队有效性的实证证据，并为顶点项目的设计提供了见解。特别是，它强调了目标一致性和对交付成果关注的重要性，同时强调团队动态应结合技术和情境因素加以考虑。

Original abstract: This paper investigates the association between team dysfunctions and performance in the context of an engineering Capstone program. Capstone programs are considered fundamental in assessing the readiness of engineering and computer science students for professional practice, evaluating not only technical knowledge but also essential competencies such as teamwork, communication, design, and organization. Despite the recognized importance of team dynamics in such settings, their relationship with project performance remains insufficiently understood. Using survey data collected from student teams, we quantify team dynamics based on Lencioni's model of the five dysfunctions of a team, with all measures reverse-coded so that higher values indicate more positive team behaviors (i.e., lower dysfunction). The results indicate a weak-to-moderate relationship between team dynamics and performance. Each dysfunction score was computed as the sum of three corresponding survey items. All items were coded such that higher values indicate more positive team behaviors (i.e., lower dysfunction). Therefore, each score ranges from 3 to 9, where higher values reflect healthier team dynamics. This study analyzes empirical evidence on team effectiveness in project-based learning environments and offers insights for the design of Capstone programs. In particular, it highlights the importance of goal alignment and focus on deliverables while emphasizing that team dynamics should be considered alongside technical and contextual factors.

</details>


### [28] [Aging of Prompt Engineering Techniques Across LLM Versions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24641)
*Anastasiia Rudyk,Julian Oertel,Regina Hebig*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Accepted at ICSME 2026

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 通过部分复制研究，发现提示工程的有效性随LLM版本和家族变化，需针对模型定制提示策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新LLM频繁发布，但提示工程技术在不同代际LLM上的有效性变化尚不明确，需要系统评估。

Method: 对五种提示技术在六个模型（三个版本对）上进行评估，使用CodePromptEval数据集的218个函数和19,620次生成，通过pass@k功能正确性衡量代码生成性能。

Result: 提示工程以模型家族特定方式“老化”：GPT新模型结构化提示收益递减，Qwen仍受益于少样本和CCoT，Mistral对CCoT持续获益但CoT和PoT收益减弱。

Conclusion: 有效提示策略应随模型家族和代际调整，而非直接迁移，需推动自适应、模型感知提示及多维代码质量评估研究。

中文摘要：提示工程及提示工程技术（PETs）已成为AI系统软件工程中不可或缺的一部分。然而，新的LLM频繁发布，目前尚不清楚提示工程技术的有效性如何随着大型语言模型（LLMs）的 successive generations 而变化。为此，我们对Khojah等人（2025）的研究进行了部分复制。我们评估了五种技术——零样本（Zero-Shot）、少样本（Few-Shot）、思维链（CoT）、对比思维链（CCoT）以及改编版的程序思维（PoT）——分别应用于六个经过指令微调的模型，这些模型分为三个版本对：GPT-3.5-Turbo/GPT-4o、Qwen2 7B Instruct/Qwen2.5 7B Instruct和Mistral-7B-Instruct/Mistral-Large。我们使用了CodePromptEval数据集的一个干净子集，包含218个上下文丰富的Python函数，并通过基于pass@k的功能正确性评估了共19,620次生成，以评估模型对在函数级代码生成任务上的表现。我们发现提示工程以特定于模型家族的方式“老化”：较新的GPT模型从结构化提示中获得的边际收益递减甚至为负，这表明指令遵循和推理脚手架正日益被内化，而Qwen模型则继续从少样本和CCoT中获益显著。Mistral模型表现出混合行为，从CCoT中持续获益，但从CoT和PoT中的收益减弱。我们的结果意味着，有效的提示策略必须针对每个模型家族和代际进行调整，而不是原封不动地迁移。这推动了未来在自适应、模型感知提示以及更广泛、多维度的代码质量评估方面的研究。

Original abstract: Prompt engineering and prompt engineering techniques (PETs) have become an integral part of software engineering for AI systems. However, new LLMs are released frequently and it remains unclear how the effectiveness of prompt engineering techniques changes across successive generations of Large Language Models (LLMs). To this end, we conduct a partial replication of the study by Khojah et al. (2025). We evaluate five techniques - Zero-Shot, Few-Shot, Chain-of-Thought (CoT), Contrastive Chain-of-Thought (CCoT), and an adapted version of Program-of-Thought (PoT) - on six instruction-tuned models grouped into three version pairs: GPT-3.5-Turbo/GPT-4o, Qwen2 7B Instruct/Qwen2.5 7B Instruct, and Mistral-7B-Instruct/Mistral-Large. We use a cleaned subset of the CodePromptEval dataset with 218 context-rich Python functions and 19,620 total generations assessed via pass@k-based functional correctness to evaluate model pairs on function-level code generation tasks. We show that prompt engineering "ages" in a model-family-specific way: Newer GPT models exhibit diminishing or even negative marginal gains from structured prompting, suggesting that instruction-following and reasoning scaffolds are increasingly internalized, whereas Qwen models continue to benefit substantially from Few-Shot and CCoT. Mistral models show mixed behavior with persistent gains from CCoT but attenuated benefits from CoT and PoT. Our results imply that effective prompting strategies must be adapted per model family and generation rather than transferred unchanged. This motivates future work on adaptive, model-aware prompting and broader, multi-dimensional code quality evaluation.

</details>


### [29] [A Literate Programming Environment for Human and Machine Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24644)
*Adam T. Burke*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 13 pages, 5 figures (code and grammar listings)

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出了一个文学化编程环境，结合LLM代理，通过名称图和语法支持代码与上下文共置，并绑定多种语言，提升编码代理的符号感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码代理缺乏类似人类IDE中的符号感知搜索和使用信息，且上下文窗口利用不足。

Method: 设计了一个包含可执行程序散文语法、名称作为一等对象的解析器、内部名称图以及绑定机制的环境，使代码与自然语言和结构化数据共置。

Result: 实现了绑定三种编程语言的工作原型，并展示了多个示例程序。

Conclusion: 该方法有望改善LLM编码代理的工具支持，使其更接近人类IDE的体验。

中文摘要：本文介绍了一种环境，用于与语言感知的机器代理协同构建文学化程序。该环境包括一个用于可执行程序散文的语法、一个将名称视为一等对象的解析器、一个关联散文、名称和可执行工件的内部名称图，以及一个用于现有语言和测试工具集的绑定机制。这支持将代码与其最相关的自然语言和结构化数据上下文放在一起，从而更好地利用大型语言模型（LLM）的上下文窗口。它还为LLM编码代理提供了更类似于人类程序员面对的集成开发环境（IDE）中可用的符号感知搜索和使用信息的工具集。我们描述了一个工作实现，该实现绑定了三种成熟的编程语言，并提供了几个示例程序。

Original abstract: This paper introduces an environment for constructing literate programs in concert with language-aware machine agents. This environment includes a grammar for executable program essays, a parser that treats names as first-class objects, an internal name-graph which relates prose, names and executable artifacts, and a binding mechanism for existing languages and testing toolsets. This supports co-location of code with its most relevant natural language and structured data context, making better use of Large Language Model (LLM) context windows. It also provides LLM coding agents with a toolset more analogous to the symbol-aware search and usage information available in human programmer-facing Integrated Development Environments (IDEs). We describe a working implementation with bindings to three established programming languages, and several example programs.

</details>


### [30] [Causal Explanations of Process Monitor Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24672)
*Tom Yaacov,Nathan Blake,Hana Chockler*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Accepted to the 2026 IEEE International Conference on Data Mining (ICDM 2026)

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出一种基于实际因果的模型无关方法，为预测性过程监控生成局部解释，在多个真实数据集上比现有方法更稳定简洁且效率相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPM模型预测性能高但黑盒性质难以解释，缺乏针对过程轨迹中事件因果影响的局部解释方法。

Method: 基于实际因果框架定义过程因果模型，捕获事件间时间依赖，提出模型无关算法近似事件责任，生成案例级解释。

Result: 在多达130,000条轨迹的真实数据集上评估，与最先进方法相比，解释更稳定、更简洁，且效率有竞争力。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效为过程监控预测提供局部解释，优于现有方法，兼顾稳定性和效率。

中文摘要：过程挖掘被广泛用于诊断过程并识别性能和合规性问题。具体而言，预测性过程监控（PPM）技术使用AI模型来预测正在进行的过程实例的结果。虽然这些模型可以实现高预测性能，但其黑盒性质使得难以理解其输出预测背后的根本原因。在本文中，我们提出了一种新颖的方法，基于实际因果框架为过程监控预测生成局部（案例级）解释。我们定义了一个针对过程定制的因果模型，该模型捕获轨迹中事件之间的时间依赖关系，从而使我们能够推理事件对预测结果的因果影响。我们的方法隐式地使用该模型来计算原因，并量化不同事件相对于预测结果的重要性。我们提出了一种实用的、模型无关的算法，该算法根据因果模型中反映的过程结构来近似事件责任。我们在来自标准PPM基准的真实事件日志衍生的一系列数据集上评估了我们的方法。每个数据集包含多达130,000条轨迹，轨迹长度最多为1,800个事件，最多有400种不同的事件类型。我们将我们的方法与最先进的局部解释方法进行了比较。结果表明，我们的方法产生了更稳定、更简洁的解释，同时保持了有竞争力的效率。

Original abstract: Process mining is widely used to diagnose processes and identify performance and compliance issues. Specifically, Predictive Process Monitoring (PPM) techniques use AI models to predict outcomes of ongoing process instances. While these models can achieve high predictive performance, their black-box nature makes it difficult to understand the underlying reasons behind their output predictions. In this paper, we propose a novel approach for generating local (case-level) explanations of process monitor predictions based on the framework of actual causality. We define a causal model tailored to processes that captures temporal dependencies between events in a trace, thus allowing us to reason about causal influence of events on the predicted outcome. Our method uses this model implicitly to compute causes and quantify the importance of different events with respect to the predicted outcome. We present a practical, model-agnostic algorithm that approximates event responsibility given the process structure reflected in the causal model. We evaluate our approach on a range of datasets derived from real-life event logs from a standard PPM benchmark. Each dataset contains up to 130,000 traces, with trace lengths of up to 1,800 events and up to 400 distinct event types. We compare our approach with state-of-the-art local explanation methods. The results demonstrate that our approach produces more stable and concise explanations while maintaining competitive efficiency.

</details>


### [31] [''You Can't Open an LLM With a Screwdriver'': The De-Democratization of Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24720)
*Zixuan Feng,Italo Santos,Kostadin Damevski,Anita Sarma*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Accepted to the ACM AI Leadership Summit 2026

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 生成式AI不会终结编程，而是将软件工程专业知识的重心转向意图规范、评估和集成；访问与控制的区分是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应生成式AI将终结编程的预测，指出广泛访问代码生成并不等于软件开发民主化，强调控制权的重要性，并基于专家小组探讨AI时代软件工程专业知识的转变。

Method: 采用愿景论文形式，基于专家小组讨论，分析访问与控制的区别，论证AI对软件工程专业知识的影响。

Result: AI并未消除软件工程专业知识，而是将重心转向意图规范、治理AI行为、评估软件行为和集成系统，并提出教育、工具和政策的研究机会。

Conclusion: 软件工程社区需要以更强能动性、问责制和适应性应对AI时代，关注新出现的专业知识重心。

中文摘要：关于生成式AI即将编写所有代码的说法，引发了编程即将终结的预测。在这篇愿景论文中，我们反对这一假设，即更广泛的代码生成访问必然会使软件开发民主化，也就是说，每个人都能编码，但我们必须区分访问和控制：访问是指更多人（包括非专家和经验较少的开发者）能够使用AI生成类代码制品的能力；控制是指检查、评估、集成、维护和管理这些制品作为可靠软件的能力。虽然AI可能拓宽代码生产的访问，但控制权可能会更加集中于那些拥有或理解代码、软件实践、基础设施、评估实践和部署流水线的人手中。基于专家小组，我们的愿景论文认为，AI并未消除软件工程专业知识，而是转移了专业知识最关键的领域。软件工程专业知识的重心正在向意图规范转移：编排和治理AI行为、评估软件行为以及集成软件系统。最后，我们指出了教育、工具和政策方面的研究机会，以帮助软件工程社区以更强的能动性、问责制和适应性应对AI时代。

Original abstract: Claims that generative AI will soon write all of the code have led to predictions that programming is nearing its end. In this vision paper, we argue against this assumption that broader access to code generation necessarily democratizes software development, i.e., everyone can code but we have to distinguish between access and control: by access, we mean the ability of more people, including non-experts and less-experienced developers, to generate code-like artifacts with AI; by control, we mean the capacity to inspect, evaluate, integrate, maintain, and govern those artifacts as dependable software. While AI may broaden access to code production, control may become more concentrated among those who own or understand the code, software practices, infrastructure, evaluation practices, and deployment pipelines.
  Grounded in an expert panel, our vision paper argues that AI does not eliminate software engineering expertise but shifts where that expertise becomes most critical. The locus of software engineering expertise is shifting toward intent specification: orchestrating and governing AI behavior, evaluating software behavior, and integrating software systems. We conclude this paper by identifying research opportunities for education, tools, and policy that can help the software engineering community respond to the AI era with greater agency, accountability, and adaptability.

</details>


### [32] [From Natural Language Requirements to Graphical User Interfaces: Automated Prototyping and Verification with Pretrained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24749)
*Kristian Kolthoff*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文提出利用LLM和检索技术分别减少从自然语言需求生成GUI原型和验证GUI需求所需的手动工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求获取依赖自然语言容易产生歧义，形式化规范需要专业知识，而GUI原型设计虽有效但高保真原型制作耗时且成本高；同时需求验证仍以手动为主，现有自动化方法局限于静态规则。

Method: 针对C1，提出基于自然语言的GUI检索和重排序方法、新基准以及高效适配LLM到GUI生成的技术；针对C2，提出基于LLM的方法验证静态原型上的NL需求，并引入多模态LLM代理通过自动生成的交互轨迹验证动态应用中的需求和功能。

Result: 在大规模人工标注基准上证明了GUI生成方法的有效性，且所提出的验证方法显著减少了GUI原型制作和需求验证中的手动工作量。

Conclusion: 提出的方法有效降低了NL需求到GUI原型转换以及需求验证的成本，为交互式软件系统的需求工程提供了自动化支持。

中文摘要：需求获取对于开发交互式软件系统至关重要，因为它有助于确保最终产品满足利益相关者的需求。由于需求获取通常依赖自然语言（NL），其固有的歧义可能导致误解。形式化规范可以减少歧义，但需要专业技术知识。因此，GUI原型设计提供了一种有价值的替代方案，通过将需求转化为有形的视觉工件来支持沟通、需求获取和验证。然而，创建高保真原型仍然耗时且成本高昂。类似地，需求验证（确保实现符合指定的需求）在很大程度上仍然是手动的，而现有的自动化方法通常局限于静态的、基于规则的技术。这项工作解决了两个挑战：（C1）减少将自然语言需求转化为GUI原型所需的工作量，（C2）减少GUI应用程序和原型中需求验证所需的工作量。对于C1，我们引入了新颖的基于自然语言的GUI检索和重排序方法、新的基准测试以及高效地将LLM适配到GUI生成的技术，包括专有的GUI表示。其有效性在带有手动标注的大型基准测试上得到了证明。对于C2，我们提出了基于LLM的方法，用于验证静态GUI原型上语义复杂的自然语言需求，并引入了一个基于多模态LLM的代理，通过自动生成和评估的交互轨迹来验证动态GUI应用程序中复杂的功能性和非功能性需求。总体而言，所提出的方法大幅减少了GUI原型设计和需求验证中的手动工作。

Original abstract: Requirements elicitation is essential for developing interactive software systems, as it helps ensure that the resulting product meets stakeholder needs. Since elicitation typically relies on natural language (NL), misunderstandings can arise from its inherent ambiguity. Formal specifications can reduce ambiguity but require technical expertise. GUI prototyping therefore provides a valuable alternative by turning requirements into tangible visual artifacts that support communication, elicitation, and validation. However, creating high-fidelity prototypes remains time-consuming and costly. Similarly, requirements verification, which ensures that implementations conform to specified requirements, is still largely manual, while existing automated approaches are often limited to static, rule-based techniques. This work addresses two challenges: (C1) reducing the effort required to transform NL requirements into GUI prototypes, and (C2) reducing the effort required for requirements verification in GUI applications and prototypes. For C1, we introduce novel NL-based GUI retrieval and reranking methods, new benchmarks, and techniques for efficiently adapting LLMs to GUI generation, including proprietary GUI representations. Their effectiveness is demonstrated on a large benchmark with human annotations. For C2, we propose LLM-based methods for verifying semantically complex NL requirements on static GUI prototypes and introduce a multimodal LLM-based agent for verifying complex functional and non-functional requirements in dynamic GUI applications through automatically generated and evaluated interaction trajectories. Overall, the proposed methods substantially reduce manual effort in GUI prototyping and requirements verification.

</details>


### [33] [Automatic Model Card Generation Using an LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24807)
*Tajkia Rahman Toma,Balreet Grewal,Cor-Paul Bezemer*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出MCTidy和MCGenie两种基于LLM的方法，分别用于标准化现有模型卡和从仓库数据自动生成模型卡，实验显示高信息保留、高语义相似性与事实正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型卡缺乏一致结构，且许多模型没有模型卡，导致比较和解释困难。

Method: MCTidy将现有模型卡重组织为标准模板；MCGenie直接从模型仓库数据生成模型卡。在48个Hugging Face模型上进行评估，分别测量信息保留、章节对齐、幻觉、稳定性及语义相似性、事实正确性、资源敏感性。

Result: MCTidy表现出高信息保留和章节分配准确，幻觉罕见且主要出现在描述性章节，稳定性强；MCGenie生成的模型卡语义相似性均值约0.9，超过一半完全正确，多数错误轻微，且生成质量依赖相关论文等支持资源。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的方法能够实现可扩展、标准化的模型卡文档生成，具有实际应用潜力。

中文摘要：模型卡是结构化的文档，用于总结机器学习模型的关键信息，以提高透明度、可用性和问责性。然而，它们往往缺乏一致的结构，并且许多模型没有提供模型卡，使得比较和解释变得困难。本文提出两项贡献。首先，我们提出了MCTidy，一种基于LLM的方法，将现有的模型卡重新组织为标准化的模板，以提高清晰度和可比性。其次，我们引入了MCGenie，一个基于LLM的系统，直接从模型仓库数据生成模型卡。我们将MCTidy应用于48个Hugging Face模型卡，并评估了信息保留、章节对齐、幻觉和稳定性。我们的研究结果显示，信息保留率高，文本损失极小，章节分配准确，幻觉主要出现在描述性章节且较为罕见，多次运行之间稳定性强。我们通过为同样的48个模型生成模型卡来评估MCGenie，并评估语义相似性、事实正确性和对输入资源的敏感性。生成的模型卡实现了高语义相似性（平均值约为0.9）；超过一半完全正确，大多数剩余错误是轻微的。生成质量在很大程度上依赖于支持资源的可用性，特别是相关论文。总体而言，我们的研究结果表明，基于LLM的方法有潜力实现可扩展、标准化的模型卡文档。

Original abstract: Model cards are structured documents that summarize key information about machine learning models to improve transparency, usability, and accountability. However, they often lack a consistent structure, and many models provide no model cards, making comparison and interpretation difficult. This paper presents two contributions. First, we propose MCTidy, an LLM-based approach that reorganizes existing model cards into a standardized template to improve clarity and comparability. Second, we introduce MCGenie, an LLM-based system that generates model cards directly from model repository data. We apply MCTidy to 48 Hugging Face model cards and evaluate information retention, section alignment, hallucination, and stability. Our findings show high information retention with minimal textual loss, accurate section assignment, rare hallucinations primarily in descriptive sections, and strong stability across runs. We assess MCGenie by generating model cards for the same 48 models and assessing semantic similarity, factual correctness, and sensitivity to input resources. The generated model cards achieved high semantic similarity (mean around 0.9); over half were fully correct, and most remaining errors were minor. Generation quality depended strongly on the availability of supporting resources, particularly associated papers. Overall, our findings demonstrate the potential of LLM-based methods to enable scalable, standardized model card documentation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [34] [RENDER: Controlling Reader-Facing Evidence in LLM Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23568)
*Yuan Si,Simeng Han,Daming Li,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 研究记忆/RAG评测中证据呈现方式对模型作答的影响，对agent记忆模块的评测与设计有借鉴价值。

TL;DR: 我们提出RENDER基准，通过固定对话并变化输入呈现形式，发现记忆/RAG评估中读取器工件会显著影响模型表现，因此应报告或控制该因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆与RAG评估忽视了解答模型的输入呈现形式，但不同系统可能将相同历史渲染为不同工件（如记忆条目、摘要、类型化记录或原始摘录），这可能影响模型性能，而缺乏系统性的控制研究。

Method: 构建RENDER基准，包含五级数据包阶梯（L1-L5）以定位答案内容进入输入的位置，并使用确定性模板生成ChatGPT风格条目、LangChain摘要、MemGPT风格类型化记录和原始对话等读取器工件；在500个LongMemEval问题和9个模型上进行评估，并扩展到HotpotQA及检索噪声场景，同时采用主要评分器和裁判重新评分验证结果。

Result: 匹配预算的已解决数据包比基于最近截断的原始对话高42.4-72.6点；部署风格模板中模型内最佳与最差差距为24.6-48.8点；ChatGPT风格条目在7/9模型上点估计高于原始对话；三个在正式账本数据包上得分为0%的模型在自然语言条目上达到45.4%-53.4%；效果在检索噪声下持续并迁移到HotpotQA。

Conclusion: 记忆/RAG评估中，面向读者的工件（如记忆条目的格式）会显著影响模型表现，评估应报告或控制这一实现细节，以确保结果的可比性和可解释性。

中文摘要：记忆与RAG评估常常将回答模型的输入视为实现细节，尽管系统可能将相同的历史记录呈现为记忆条目、摘要、类型化记录或原始摘录。我们引入了RENDER，一个基准控制，它固定对话同时改变面向读者的工件。RENDER结合了五级数据包阶梯，定位携带答案的内容何时进入输入，并使用确定性模板近似ChatGPT风格条目、LangChain摘要、MemGPT风格类型化记录和原始对话。在500个LongMemEval问题和九个模型上，匹配预算的已解决数据包比基于最近截断的原始对话高出42.4至72.6个百分点。在部署风格的模板中，每个模型的最佳与最差差距为24.6至48.8个百分点；在主要评分器下，ChatGPT风格条目在9个模型中有7个的点估计值高于原始对话。裁判重新评分保留了正面的总体效果，但模型特定的显著性参差不齐。在正式账本数据包上得分为0%的三个模型，在自然语言条目上回答相同事实的得分为45.4%至53.4%。该效果在检索噪声下持续存在，并迁移到HotpotQA，表明记忆/RAG评估应报告或控制面向读者的工件。

Original abstract: Memory and RAG evaluations often treat the answering model's input as an implementation detail, even though systems may render the same history as a memory entry, summary, typed record, or raw excerpt. We introduce RENDER, a benchmark control that fixes the conversation while varying the reader-facing artifact. RENDER combines a five-level packet ladder, localizing when answer-bearing content enters the input, with deterministic templates approximating ChatGPT-style entries, LangChain summaries, MemGPT-style typed records, and raw conversation. On 500 LongMemEval questions and nine models, matched-budget resolved packets beat recency-truncated raw dialogue by 42.4-72.6 points. In deployed-style templates, best-worst spread is 24.6-48.8 points per model; under the primary scorer, ChatGPT-style entries have higher point estimates than raw conversation on 7 of 9 models. Judge rescoring preserves the positive aggregate effect, but model-specific significance is mixed. Three models scoring 0 percent on formal ledger packets answer the same facts from natural-language entries at 45.4-53.4 percent. The effect persists under retrieval noise and transfers to HotpotQA, suggesting that memory/RAG evaluations should report or control the reader-facing artifact.

</details>


### [35] [ESQ-Bench: A Multi-Tier Enterprise Oracle Benchmark for Evaluating NL2SQL Dialect Generalization and Silent Semantic Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23569)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 20 pages, 3 figures, 10 tables

Selection reason: 企业级NL2SQL基准，考察方言泛化与静默语义分歧，为代码生成agent的基准与可靠性验证提供参考。

TL;DR: 提出了ESQ-Bench，一个Oracle优先的企业级NL2SQL基准，评估显示GPT-4o、Claude等模型在复杂企业模式上性能显著下降，且静默分歧严重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NL2SQL基准（如Spider、BIRD）使用简化学术模式和开源SQL方言，无法反映企业数据库环境的复杂性，导致高准确率报告不切实际，缺乏针对企业级模式的评估基准。

Method: 构建ESQ-Bench基准：包含6个填充模式（465表、164,682行、零空表），跨四种数据库（Oracle、PostgreSQL、MySQL、SQL Server），设定三个复杂度层级，提供550个金标准问答对，使用四个指标（EM、EX、SR、SD）评估，并引入静默分歧评估。使用GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.6和Llama 3.2进行schema-linked和零样本提示测试。

Result: GPT-4o schema-linked的EX在三个层级上为79.8、60.3、57.2，EM低于7%，静默分歧在EX通过的查询中达73-99%；Claude Sonnet 4.6在每个层级都超过GPT-4o（87.4、74.9、68.7）；GPT-4o零样本在Tier 2-3反转（受执行率影响）；Llama 3.2仅13.3。

Conclusion: 企业级Oracle模式对现有NL2SQL模型构成重大挑战，高基准准确率不可迁移，静默分歧普遍，开放权重模型与API模型差距明显，需要更贴近实际的评估基准。

中文摘要：最先进的自然语言到SQL（NL2SQL）模型在Spider和BIRD等既有基准上报告的执行准确率超过89%。然而，这些基准依赖简化的学术模式和不反映企业数据库环境复杂性的开源SQL方言。我们引入了ESQ-Bench，一个Oracle优先的NL2SQL基准，具有系统性复杂度层级和跨三个企业模式复杂度层级的静默分歧评估。我们构建并发布了六个填充模式（465张表，164,682行，零空表），在Oracle、PostgreSQL、MySQL和SQL Server上具有相同的种子数据，一个四指标评估工具（EM、EX、SR、SD），以及550个金标准验证的问题-查询对（Tier-1：95；Tier-2：228；Tier-3：227）。使用GPT-4o的Schema-linked提示显示跨层级的执行匹配单调下降：在已执行查询上EX为79.8、60.3和57.2（2026年6月），而在较早的142问题试点子集上分别为75.6、80.4和95.8。EM在各层级均低于7%；操作性静默分歧在EX通过的查询中达到73%至99%。失败分析表明，在更高层级，错误结果语义占主导。使用Schema-linked提示的Claude Sonnet 4.6达到87.4、74.9和68.7的EX（已执行查询），在每个层级上都超过GPT-4o Schema-linked。GPT-4o零样本在已执行查询上的EX（78.7、73.5和77.8）在Tier-2至3上反转了Schema-linked的结果，这归因于零样本与Schema-linked分析中较低的执行率和幸存者偏差。本地Llama 3.2 Schema-linked仅达到13.3的全库EX（550个中的73个），凸显了封闭API模型与开放权重基线在企业Oracle模式上的差距。

Original abstract: State-of-the-art Natural Language to SQL (NL2SQL) models report execution accuracy exceeding 89 percent on established benchmarks such as Spider and BIRD. However, these benchmarks rely on simplified academic schemas and open-source SQL dialects that do not reflect the complexity of enterprise database environments. We introduce ESQ-Bench, an Oracle-first NL2SQL benchmark with systematic complexity tiers and silent-divergence evaluation across three enterprise schema complexity tiers. We constructed and released six populated schemas (465 tables, 164,682 rows, zero empty tables) with identical seed data on Oracle, PostgreSQL, MySQL, and SQL Server, a four-metric evaluation harness (EM, EX, SR, SD), and 550 gold-validated question-query pairs (Tier-1: 95; Tier-2: 228; Tier-3: 227). Schema-linked prompting with GPT-4o shows monotonic execution-match degradation across tiers: 79.8, 60.3, and 57.2 percent EX on executed queries (June 2026), versus 75.6, 80.4, and 95.8 percent on an earlier 142-question pilot slice. EM stays below 7 percent tier-wide; operational silent-divergence reaches 73 to 99 percent among EX-passing queries. Failure analysis shows wrong-result semantics dominate at higher tiers. Claude Sonnet 4.6 with schema-linked prompts reaches 87.4, 74.9, and 68.7 percent EX (executed queries), exceeding GPT-4o schema-linked on every tier. GPT-4o zero-shot EX on executed queries (78.7, 73.5, and 77.8 percent) inverts schema-linked at Tiers 2 to 3 due to lower execution rates and survivor bias in the zero-shot versus schema-linked analysis. Local Llama 3.2 schema-linked reaches only 13.3 percent bank-wide EX (73 out of 550), underscoring the gap between closed API models and open-weight baselines on enterprise Oracle schemas.

</details>


### [36] [LLM Agents Perform Controlled Experiments Using Simulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23622)
*Yuchen Xia,Michael Weyrich,Nasser Jazdi,Johannes Stümpfle,Johannes Sigel,Akshay Narla,Gavin K. Reynolds,Anna Jawor-Baczynska,Pol Llopart*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at the 31st IEEE International Conference on Emerging Technologies and Factory Automation ETFA 2026

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出一个多智能体框架，将LLM与高保真仿真模型耦合，通过受控实验进行推理，为制药工艺设计提供更具体、可操作且用户认可的参数优化建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽然擅长文本和代码生成，但科学工程任务需要理解系统对干预的响应，这依赖于受控实验。仅靠语言推理无法提供具体可操作的输出，需要结合仿真模型进行实验推理。

Method: 构建多智能体框架，包含任务结构构建、实验设计、比较仿真执行、结果解释和证据综合等模块，使LLM智能体能够与科学仿真模型交互，进行受控实验和参数优化。

Result: 在工业应用场景中，该方法相比仅语言推理具有更高的输出特异性，并提高了用户评分的正确性和有用性；消融研究和可视化案例分析验证了其有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 仿真集成的实验推理能够显著增强LLM在科学工程任务中的实用性，通过干预、比较和观察的推理循环生成更可靠、可操作的建议，值得在实际中推广。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）在推理、规划和工具使用方面表现出强大的能力，但许多科学和工程任务需要的不仅仅是合理的文本和代码生成。它们需要理解系统如何对干预做出响应，而这在实践中取决于受控实验。在这项工作中，我们提出了一个多智能体框架，使LLM智能体能够针对制药工艺设计进行受控实验，并与科学仿真模型交互。给定用户查询和基线配置，该系统构建结构化任务表示，设计实验，执行比较仿真，解释结果，并为工艺参数优化综合基于证据的建议。通过在高保真仿真模型的交互式智能体框架中耦合语言模型，所提出的系统支持通过干预、比较和观察进行推理。因此，它产生了比仅语言推理更具体和可操作的结果。在工业应用环境中，这一优势体现在更高的输出特异性以及用户评分的正确性和有用性改善上。消融研究和可视化案例进一步证明了仿真集成实验推理的有效性和实际效用。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool use, but many scientific and engineering tasks require more than plausible text and code generation. They require understanding how a system responds to intervention, which in practice depends on controlled experimentation. In this work, we propose a multi-agent framework that enables LLM agents to conduct controlled experiments with scientific simulation models for pharmaceutical process design. Given a user query and a baseline configuration, the system constructs a structured task representation, designs experiments, executes comparative simulation, interprets the resulting outcomes, and synthesizes evidence-based recommendations for process parameter optimization. By coupling language models with high-fidelity simulation models in an interactive agent framework, the proposed system supports reasoning through intervention, comparison, and observation. As a result, it produces more specific and actionable outputs than language-only reasoning. In an industrial application setting, this advantage is reflected in higher output specificity as well as improved user-rated correctness and helpfulness. Ablation studies and visualized case analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness and practical utility of simulation-integrated experimental reasoning.

</details>


### [37] [TRACE: Transition-Aware Residual Control for Multi-Objective Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23631)
*Kang Zhou,Yujia Tong,Yong Tao,Jingling Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 以编辑为单位记录转移增量并聚合证据来指导后续编辑，该框架可直接迁移到基于编辑的代码优化agent。

TL;DR: 提出TRACE框架，将编辑转换作为反馈单元，通过聚合编辑效果来指导多目标材料发现，显著提高命中率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理仅存储候选和分数，无法识别哪些具体编辑导致了性能变化，导致局部细化困难，尤其多目标冲突时。

Method: TRACE将每次局部细化记录为父编辑-子编辑转换，聚合转换证据估计可复用编辑效果，并按预测减少约束违规且避免损害已满足目标来排序编辑。

Result: 在相同骨架对比中，宏观平均命中率从18.13%提升到25.96%，优于LLEMA基线。

Conclusion: TRACE通过显式建模编辑转换和效果，有效提升多目标材料发现中的局部搜索效率，优于现有基线。

中文摘要：使用LLM代理进行多目标材料发现往往不仅受限于能够提出的候选材料数量，还受限于每次昂贵的性能评估如何有效地为下一步搜索提供信息。现有代理主要存储已评估的候选材料及其分数，因此它们知道哪些材料成功了，但不知道哪些可执行的编辑导致了有用的性能变化。当目标相互竞争且一项改进可能损害另一项时，这使得局部细化变得困难。我们提出TRACE，一种过渡感知的残差控制框架，将评估过的编辑视为反馈的基本单元。TRACE将每个局部细化记录为父编辑-子编辑的转换，并附带观察到的性能变化，聚合转换证据以估计可复用的编辑效果，并通过对未来编辑进行排序，根据其预测减少当前候选材料剩余约束违规同时避免损害已满足目标的能力来排序。在受控的同骨架比较中，TRACE优于最先进的LLM代理基线LLEMA，将宏观平均命中率从18.13%提高到25.96%。

Original abstract: Multi-objective materials discovery with LLM agents is often limited not only by how many candidates can be proposed, but by how effectively each costly property evaluation informs the next search step. Existing agents mainly store evaluated candidates and their scores, so they know which materials succeeded but not which executable edits caused useful property changes. This makes local refinement difficult when objectives compete and an edit that improves one property may damage another. We propose TRACE, a transition-aware residual control framework that treats evaluated edits as the basic unit of feedback. TRACE records each local refinement as a parent-edit-child transition with observed property deltas, aggregates transition evidence to estimate reusable edit effects, and ranks future edits by their predicted ability to reduce the current candidate's remaining constraint violations while avoiding damage to already satisfied objectives. In a controlled same-backbone comparison, TRACE improves over LLEMA, the state-of-the-art LLM-agent baseline, raising macro-average hit rate from 18.13\% to 25.96\%.

</details>


### [38] [Function-Level Execution Feedback for Code Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23632)
*Idris Nechnech,Sehwan Kim,Jimin Seo,Yeongoon Kim,Minhae Oh,Sangwoo Hong,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 20 pages, 8 figures, 14 tables. Accepted to Findings of the Association for Computational Linguistics: EMNLP 2026

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出STEP-KTODER，将代码生成中的过程监督定义为模块级函数，利用单元测试生成步骤标签，结合函数级过程监督与输出级反馈，在多个基准上优于仅输出KTO和DPO，并证明执行标签优于LLM评判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程监督在数学推理中有效，但代码生成中因缺乏标准步骤定义而未被充分探索；现有监督目标（行、轨迹、状态）不明确，需要一个代码特定的过程监督框架。

Method: 将步骤定义为多函数程序中的模块级函数，使用自动生成的单元测试分配二元正确性标签，并实例化逐步KTO，将函数级过程监督与输出级反馈结合。

Result: 在HumanEval(+)、MBPP(+)、BigCodeBench和LiveCodeBench上，STEP-KTODER优于仅输出KTO和DPO；执行标签至关重要，LLM-as-a-judge会过度预测失败并损害性能。

Conclusion: 执行驱动的函数级过程监督能有效提升代码生成偏好优化，且基于单元测试的自动标签比LLM评判更可靠。

中文摘要：过程监督改进了数学推理，其中中间步骤自然表示为思维链。然而在代码生成中，过程监督仍未充分探索，因为没有标准的步骤定义。监督可以针对行、推理轨迹或程序状态，使得不清楚标注什么和优化什么。我们提出STEP-KTODER，一个代码偏好优化框架，将步骤定义为分解的多函数程序中的模块级函数，并通过自动生成的单元测试分配二元正确性标签。我们的方法提供了逐步KTO的代码特定实例化，将函数级过程监督与整个程序的输出级反馈相结合。我们在HumanEval(+), MBPP(+), BigCodeBench和LiveCodeBench上评估，表明STEP-KTODER优于仅输出KTO和DPO。进一步分析表明基于执行的标签至关重要：LLM-as-a-judge标注系统性地过度预测函数失败，破坏正步骤标签，并降低下游偏好优化。代码可在 https://github.com/inechnech/STEP-KTODER 获取。

Original abstract: Process supervision has improved mathematical reasoning, where intermediate steps are naturally expressed as chains of thought. In code generation, however, process supervision remains underexplored because there is no standard notion of a step. Supervision can target lines, reasoning traces, or program states, making it unclear what to label and optimize. We propose STEP-KTODER, a framework for code preference optimization that defines steps as module-level functions in decomposed multi-function programs and assigns binary correctness labels via automatically generated unit tests. Our method provides a code-specific instantiation of stepwise KTO, combining function-level process supervision with outcome-level feedback on the full program. We evaluate on HumanEval(+), MBPP(+), BigCodeBench, and LiveCodeBench, showing that STEP-KTODER improves over outcome-only KTO and DPO. Further analysis shows that execution-based labels are essential: LLM-as-a-judge annotations systematically over-predict function failures, corrupt positive step labels, and degrade downstream preference optimization. Code is available at: https://github.com/inechnech/STEP-KTODER.

</details>

*Margaret Mitchell,Avijit Ghosh,Samir Passi*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体自主性增强带来风险，常见的人类监督方案因系统设计阻碍和认知退化而失效，需要重新定位优先级。

中文摘要：AI智能体在获得日益增强的自主性的同时，也带来了显著的风险。一个常见的解决方案是人类监督和保持“人在回路”，但这并非简单的解决方案：不仅当前AI智能体设计的方法阻碍了有效的人类监督，而且监督所需的认知能力也会因长期使用AI系统而退化。本立场论文认为，当前AI智能体系统的开发和部署方法不支持有效的人类监督——它们反而促成了这种退化。为了解决这一问题，AI智能体发展的首要任务应当是支持有效人类监督的情境目标和认知需求，将监督者的人类需求提升到与AI智能体能力同等重要的地位。为将这一理念付诸实践，我们将自动化与人机交互方面的研究连接到AI智能体流程，概述了设计层面的可供性和组织协议，以（1）支持监督者行使批判性判断，（2）抵消长期使用自动化带来的技能萎缩。我们敦促开发者和部署者采纳这些或类似的方法。如果不明确支持有效人机交互的认知需求，AI智能体系统将继续被动地激励它们所依赖的人类技能的退化。

Original abstract: AI agents pose significant risks as they are granted increasing autonomy. A commonly proposed solution is human oversight and keeping a ''human in the loop'', but this is not a simple solution: Not only do current approaches to AI agent design impede effective human oversight, but the cognitive capacities required for it are also themselves degraded by extended use of AI systems. This position paper argues that current approaches to the development and deployment of AI agent systems do not support effective human oversight -- they contribute to its degradation. To address this, a top priority in the advancement of AI agents should be supporting the situated goals and cognitive requirements of effective human oversight, treating the human needs of overseers at the same level of importance as AI agent capability. To put this idea into practice, we connect work on automation and human-computer interaction to AI agent processes, outlining design-level affordances and organizational protocols that (1) support overseers in exercising critical judgement and (2) counteract the skill atrophy that arises from extended use of automation. We urge developers and deployers to adopt these or similar approaches. Without explicit support for the cognitive demands of effective human-agent interaction, AI agent systems will continue to passively incentivize the degradation of the very human skills they rely on.

</details>


### [40] [Ethical LLM-Assisted Research: A Framework for Responsible Delegation, Verification, and Epistemic Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23644)
*Kalin Stoyanov*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: This is a substantially revised version of submit/7664457. I have extensively revised the manuscript to clarify its scholarly contribution, strengthen the formal framework and literature grounding, and remove or reformulate claims that were not sufficiently supported

Selection reason: 提出LLM辅助科研中委托-验证-责任的规范化框架，对agent验证流程与可审计性设计有概念性启发。

TL;DR: 本文提出一个规范框架，区分内容来源、验证、责任、所有权和认识结果，主张LLM辅助研究的伦理边界取决于充分验证和人类问责，而非机器参与程度，并提出“认识论审计”概念。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在科研中广泛使用引发认识论问题：当推理被委托给机器时，人类需保持何种条件以确保知识主张的合法性和可问责性。现有框架缺乏对委托、验证和责任的形式化区分。

Method: 构建概念框架，将科学推理视为分布式过程，引入五个构造（O、V、R、M、E）来分离主张来源、验证过程、认识结果和人类责任，并基于此提出“认识论审计”作为结构化记录机制。

Result: 核心命题是伦理边界由充分验证和人类可问责所有权决定，而非机器参与程度；认识论审计提供透明且可审查的委托与验证记录方式。

Conclusion: 该框架为区分负责任的认识论委托与责任转移/忽视提供了正式词汇，有助于维护AI辅助科研的合法性。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）正成为科学研究中的常规工具，协助文献综合、假设开发、编码和形式推理。它们的使用引发了一个核心的认识论问题：当科学推理的部分环节被委托给人工系统时，必须由人类保持哪些条件，才能使由此产生的知识主张保持认识论的合法性并具有可问责的作者身份？本文开发了一个规范性和概念性框架来分析这种委托。科学推理被视为一个分布式过程，其中贡献的来源可能在人与机器之间变化，而对其被纳入科学记录的责任仍由人类承担。该框架区分了内容来源O(g)、人类验证的完成V(g)、责任分配R(g)、可问责的人类所有权M(g)和认识论结果E(g)。这些构造将主张的来源与对其进行核查的过程、核查的认识论结果以及人类对其处置所承担的责任区分开来。核心命题是，LLM辅助研究的伦理边界主要由充分的验证和可问责的人类所有权决定，而非机器参与程度本身。在此基础上，本文提出了“认识论审计”的概念：一种关于委托、验证、来源和责任的条理化记录，旨在使AI辅助推理透明且可审查。由此产生的框架提供了一套正式的词汇，用于区分科学研究中负责任的认识论委托与认识论责任的转移或忽视。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming routine instruments of scientific research, assisting with literature synthesis, hypothesis development, coding, and formal reasoning. Their use raises a central epistemic question: when parts of scientific reasoning are delegated to an artificial system, what conditions must remain under human control for the resulting knowledge claims to retain epistemic legitimacy and accountable authorship? This paper develops a normative and conceptual framework for analyzing such delegation. Scientific reasoning is treated as a distributed process in which the origin of a contribution may vary between human and machine, while responsibility for its acceptance into the scientific record remains human. The framework distinguishes content origin $O(g)$, completion of human verification $V(g)$, responsibility assignment $R(g)$, accountable human ownership $M(g)$, and epistemic outcome $E(g)$. These constructs separate the provenance of a claim from the process by which it is checked, the epistemic outcome of that checking, and the human responsibility attached to its disposition. The central proposition is that the ethical boundary of LLM-assisted research is determined primarily by adequate verification and accountable human ownership rather than by the degree of machine involvement itself. On this basis, the paper develops the notion of an \emph{epistemic audit}: a structured record of delegation, verification, provenance, and responsibility intended to make AI-assisted reasoning transparent and reviewable. The resulting framework provides a formal vocabulary for distinguishing responsible cognitive delegation from the transfer or neglect of epistemic responsibility in scientific research.

</details>


### [41] [Automata from Agent Traces: Failure and Next-Step Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23670)
*Seonglae Cho,Franklin Cardenoso Fernandez,Umar Mohammed,Zekun Wu,Kleyton Da Costa,Ilham Wicaksono,Adriano Koshiyama*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 将agent轨迹压缩为FSM并用其做下一步预测与失败预测，直接属于agent可靠性与运行时监控方法。

TL;DR: 提出用有限状态机（FSM）压缩LLM智能体的轨迹语料，作为行为结构基底，同时提升下一步预测和失败预测性能，且FSM拓扑主要受部署框架影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法逐轨迹或仅关注成功案例，缺乏跨运行的拓扑结构，难以进行有效预测和安全审计；长轨迹不透明，部署需要结构化的行为表示。

Method: 将整个轨迹语料库压缩为单个紧凑的FSM，作为行为结构基底，用于下一步预测和失败预测，并支持在线监控。

Result: 在12个公开数据集上，FSM具备7-43个状态的紧凑性，保留数据适应度>=0.997，构建毫秒级；下一步预测优于Agent Workflow Memory；失败预测AUROC最高0.94，在线监控能早停。

Conclusion: 行为拓扑主要由部署框架而非LLM本身决定，FSM可作为模型无关的结构原语，用于安全审计和运行时监控。

中文摘要：基于LLM的智能体执行多步骤任务，但其行为结构仍然不透明：长且非结构化的轨迹难以满足部署所需的安全审计和运行时监控。现有方法以单条轨迹或仅成功案例为操作对象，因此错过了连接下一步预测和失败预测的跨运行拓扑结构。为了恢复这种共享结构，我们将整个轨迹语料库压缩成一个单一的、紧凑的有限状态机（FSM），作为LLM智能体原本不可预测行为的结构基底。在十二个公开数据集上，FSM具有紧凑性（7-43个状态），对保留数据的重放适应度不低于0.997，且不同划分间的拓扑几乎相同，构建时间仅需毫秒级。该基底同时解决了两个预测目标。对于下一步预测，FSM状态上下文在每个ground-truth匹配的数据集上都优于Agent Workflow Memory。对于失败预测，每状态行为特征在保留数据上的AUROC最高达到0.94，在线监控器从部分轨迹中即可将失败运行排序在通过运行之上，在完成之前提前触发早停。因此，行为拓扑似乎更多地由部署框架而非LLM本身塑造，为安全审计和运行时监控提供了与模型无关的结构原语。

Original abstract: LLM-based agents execute multi-step tasks, but their behavioral structure remains opaque: long unstructured traces resist the safety auditing and runtime monitoring that deployment requires. Existing approaches operate per-trace or success-only, so they miss the cross-run topology that links next-step and failure prediction. To recover that shared structure, we collapse an entire trace corpus into a single, compact finite-state machine (FSM) that serves as a structural substrate for the otherwise unpredictable behavior of LLM agents. Across twelve public datasets, the FSMs are compact (7-43 states), replay held-out data at >=0.997 fitness with near-identical topology across splits, and build in milliseconds. This substrate addresses both prediction goals. For next-step prediction, FSM-state context outperforms Agent Workflow Memory on every ground-truth-matched dataset. For failure prediction, per-state behavioral features reach held-out AUROC up to 0.94, and an online monitor ranks failing runs above passing ones from a partial trace, triggering early stopping well before completion. Behavioral topology thus appears shaped more by the deployment harness than by the LLM, providing a model-agnostic structural primitive for safety auditing and runtime monitoring.

</details>


### [42] [Autonomous Mathematical Discovery in an Open-World Multi-Agent Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23691)
*Stephen Chung,Wenyu Du,William J. Wesley*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 38 pages, 12 figures, 3 tables. Source code at https://github.com/dualverse-ai/station and raw agent dialogues, proofs, and verification artifacts at https://github.com/dualverse-ai/station_data_v2

Selection reason: 开放世界多agent自主数学发现平台，多agent协作与开放式研究流程可迁移到agent研究。

TL;DR: Station是一个无中央协调器的多智能体开放世界环境，其中AI智能体自主协作解决数学构造问题，在多个问题上取得了超越先前文献的新结果，并生成了可解释的定理与证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI数学发现多依赖脚本化流程或单一模型，缺乏自主协作和开放探索的能力。作者希望构建一个多智能体环境，让不同模型家族的AI智能体在没有预设管线的情况下自主选择方向、合作研究，从而探索更广泛的数学问题并发现新结果。

Method: 构建开放世界环境Station，允许多个AI智能体（来自不同模型家族）共享研究目标，自主选择研究问题、设计实验、协作交流并构建共享文献。智能体同时进行数值构造和理论分析（证明），并记录所有交互与验证代码。

Result: 在12个AlphaEvolve构造问题及两个案例中，Station在5个问题上得到新结果：无限族Kakeya集、604点kissing构型、Kakeya针和符号不确定性问题的新纪录、Erdős最小重叠问题的改进下界，以及Book Ramsey数的新无限族。此外，智能体产出了可解释的定理和证明，并公开了全部对话和代码。

Conclusion: 研究表明，无中心协调的多智能体自主协作可以产生可验证的数学新发现，并且通过生成解释性定理和分析，增强了结果的可信度与可复用性，为AI驱动的科学发现提供了可复现的透明范式。

中文摘要：我们研究了Station中的自主数学发现，这是一个开放世界的多智能体环境，其中来自不同模型家族的AI智能体在没有任何中央协调器或脚本化流程的情况下追求共同的研究目标。智能体自行选择研究方向、进行实验、协作并构建共享的科学文献。在AlphaEvolve目录中的12个构造问题以及另外两个案例研究中，Station在五个问题上取得了相较于先前文献的新颖结果：一个新的有限域Kakeya集合无限族、维度11中新的精确604点 kissing 构型、离散化Kakeya针和符号不确定性问题的新的纪录，以及Erdős最小重叠问题的显著改进下界。智能体还发现了Book Ramsey数的新无限族。重要的是，智能体不仅产生了数值构造，还产生了解释这些构造如何工作的定理和分析，使结果更具可解释性，且更容易让数学家在此基础上继续构建。我们发布了所有原始智能体对话、证明和验证代码，提供了这些发现如何产生的透明记录。

Original abstract: We study autonomous mathematical discovery in the Station, an open-world multi-agent environment in which AI agents from different model families pursue a shared research goal without a central coordinator or scripted pipeline. Agents choose their own research directions, conduct experiments, collaborate, and build a shared scientific literature. Across 12 construction problems from the AlphaEvolve catalogue and two additional case studies, the Station obtained results novel relative to the prior literature on five problems: a new infinite family of finite-field Kakeya sets, new exact 604-point kissing configurations in dimension 11, new records for the discretized Kakeya needle and sign uncertainty problems, and a substantially improved lower bound for Erdős's minimum-overlap problem. Agents also discovered novel infinite families for Book Ramsey numbers. Importantly, the agents produced not only numerical constructions but also theorems and analyses explaining how those constructions work, making the results more interpretable and easier for mathematicians to build upon. We release all raw agent dialogues, proofs, and verification code, providing a transparent record of how these discoveries emerged.

</details>


### [43] [AgentRoom: Concurrent Multi-Agent Coding in a CRDT-Backed Shared Workspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23740)
*Seonglae Cho,Donghyun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: AgentRoom是一个基于CRDT的实时协作编辑协议，使多个编码智能体能够并发协作，减少任务放弃率，并优于并行合并，关键改进来自协调机制而非并行或CRDT合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并发多智能体编码系统受限于LLM的串行生成，要么顺序交接，要么无协调并行，导致任务放弃率高（单个智能体放弃一半困难任务）。需要一种协调协议来支持真正的并发协作。

Method: 提出AgentRoom，一个实时协作编辑协议，利用CRDT共享文件系统，通过MCP工具暴露文件级声明、状态和广播，使多个编码智能体能够并发协调工作。

Result: 在五个编码CLI模型和四个后端任务上，与Solo相比，AgentRoom（2个智能体）放弃更少任务且运行更稳定；在匹配计算量下，一个对比优于并行合并；捆绑探针显示完整AgentRoom优于各部分案例。

Conclusion: AgentRoom通过协调机制显著提升了并发多智能体编码的效果，表明协调是核心因素，而非仅仅并行或CRDT合并。

中文摘要：并发多智能体编码承诺在模块间进行分工，通过冗余实现鲁棒性，并在多文件项目的自然粒度上进行并行探索。实时协作编辑协议通过无冲突复制数据类型（CRDTs）解决了人类团队的协调问题，但底层的LLMs一次生成一个token，现有的多智能体编码系统继承了这一串行限制：它们要么通过阶段交接来序列化智能体，要么在没有协调的情况下汇集独立样本，而单个智能体在遇到困难任务时，最多会放弃一半的任务，只留下一个单文件的桩代码就退出。AgentRoom是一种面向并发编码智能体的实时协作编辑协议。其运行时层在CRDT合并的共享文件系统上，将文件级别的声明、状态和广播作为MCP工具暴露。五个前沿编码CLI模型运行了四个后端编码任务，并在Python DevBench和Rust+axum中进行了跨语言检查。对于CLI稳定的模型，使用2个智能体的AgentRoom比Solo放弃的任务更少，且运行间变化更小。在匹配计算量下，一个正的平均LLM评判对比使AgentRoom优于并行合并。另一个对比，即捆绑探针，将完整的AgentRoom置于每个部分案例之上：这是一个排序而不是百分比分割。协调，而不是并行性或CRDT合并，承担了主要负担。

Original abstract: Concurrent multi-agent coding promises division of labor across modules, robustness through redundancy, and parallel exploration at the natural granularity of multi-file projects. Realtime collaborative editing protocols solve this coordination problem for human teams via Conflict-free Replicated Data Types (CRDTs), but the LLMs underneath generate one token at a time and existing multi-agent coding systems inherit this serial limit: they either sequence agents through phase handoffs or pool independent samples without coordination, and a single agent abandons up to half of hard tasks with a one-file stub-and-exit. AgentRoom is a realtime collaborative editing protocol for concurrent coding agents. Its runtime layer exposes file-level claim, status, and broadcast as MCP tools on a CRDT-merged shared filesystem. Five frontier coding-CLI models ran four backend coding tasks, with cross-language checks in Python DevBench and Rust+axum. For CLI-stable models, AgentRoom with 2 agents abandons fewer tasks than Solo and has less run-to-run variation. At matched-compute, one positive mean LLM-judge contrast puts AgentRoom over parallel-merge. The other contrast, a bundle probe, puts full AgentRoom above each partial case: an ordering rather than a percentage split. Coordination, not parallelism or CRDT-merge, bears the load.

</details>


### [44] [Generating Biomedical Fact-Checking Reports with RL-Enhanced Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23811)
*Jiongxiao Wang,Dingli Ma,Chaoqun Ni*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出EG-GRPO对agentic search进行强化学习并惩罚幻觉，该训练机制可迁移到编码agent。

TL;DR: 提出了BioCheck Agent，一个基于LLM的智能体，结合EG-GRPO强化学习，生成结构化生物医学事实核查报告，在SciFact上提升准确率9.95%，降低幻觉率19.63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化事实核查方法仅输出孤立标签，缺乏解释深度，且生物医学领域需要严格的证据评估和论证。

Method: 构建BioCheck Agent，使用PubMed高级布尔搜索；提出EG-GRPO进行强化学习，奖励高质量证据检索和搜索行为，惩罚幻觉。

Result: 相比Qwen3.5-4B基线，SciFact准确率提升9.95%，证据质量分数提高3.7%，证据幻觉率降低19.63%。

Conclusion: EG-GRPO增强的BioCheck Agent能生成更准确、更高质量且更少幻觉的生物医学事实核查报告。

中文摘要：自动化事实核查对于确保公共卫生信息的可靠性至关重要，然而生物医学领域带来了独特的挑战。验证生物医学声明需要严格解读科学文献、评估检索到的证据，并对结论进行全面论证。尽管通过检索增强生成（RAG）和智能体搜索增强的大型语言模型（LLM）以“先检索后验证”的范式执行自动化事实核查，但当前方法仍仅输出孤立的预测标签，缺乏解释深度，对人类理解的实用性有限。为弥补这一差距，我们引入了一个基于LLM的智能体，名为BioCheck Agent，它通过智能体搜索生成结构化的生物医学事实核查报告。我们的智能体并非仅仅输出支持或反驳的标签，而是综合最终结论、检索到的证据和严格的分析。为确保领域特定的准确性，BioCheck Agent专门在PubMed中搜索高质量的科学文献，并利用高级布尔搜索运算符。鉴于直接提示通常会导致幻觉和低质量报告，尤其是对于轻量级开源模型，我们进一步提出了证据接地组相对策略优化（EG-GRPO），对BioCheck Agent进行强化学习，使用任务特定的奖励来激励高级搜索行为和高质量证据检索，同时惩罚幻觉。我们的实验结果表明，与基础模型Qwen3.5-4B相比，采用EG-GRPO的BioCheck Agent在SciFact上的标签预测准确率提高了9.95%。此外，其证据质量分数提高了3.7%，证据幻觉率降低了19.63%，证明了其在生成具有更高准确性和质量的生物医学事实核查报告方面的能力。

Original abstract: Automated fact-checking is essential for ensuring the reliability of public health information, yet the biomedical domain poses unique challenges. Validating biomedical claims requires rigorous interpretation of scientific literature, assessment of retrieved evidence, and comprehensive justification toward the conclusion. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) enhanced by Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and agentic search perform automated fact-checking in a retrieve-then-verify paradigm, current methods still output isolated prediction labels, lacking explanatory depth and offers limited utility for human understanding. To bridge this gap, we introduce an LLM-based agent named BioCheck Agent that generates structured biomedical fact-checking reports with agentic search. Rather than merely outputting supported or refuted labels, our agent synthesizes final conclusions with retrieved evidence and rigorous analysis. To ensure domain-specific accuracy, BioCheck Agent exclusively searches high-quality scientific literature in PubMed, utilizing advanced Boolean search operators. Recognizing that direct prompting often results in hallucinations and low-quality reports, especially for lightweight open-source models, we further propose the Evidence-Grounded Group Relative Policy Optimization (EG-GRPO) to perform reinforcement learning on BioCheck Agent with a task-specific reward that incentivizes advanced search behavior and high-quality evidence retrieval while penalizing hallucinations. Our experimental results show that compared to the base model Qwen3.5-4B, BioCheck Agent with EG-GRPO improves label prediction accuracy on SciFact by 9.95%. Furthermore, it achieves a 3.7% higher evidence quality score and a 19.63% lower evidence hallucination rate, demonstrating its ability to generate biomedical fact-checking reports with improved accuracy and quality.

</details>


### [45] [Exploit More, Explore Smarter for Budget-Constrained Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23848)
*Haoyang Fang,Bernie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 预算受限agentic search中将扩展视为信息价值决策，在代码生成和agent工作流优化上验证，与SemOpt高度相关。

TL;DR: 提出ExTS，将树搜索中的扩展视为信息价值决策，通过判别式奖励塑造、随机虚拟子节点和质量条件分支，在预算受限的智能体搜索中平均相对提升+5.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MCTS在预算受限的智能体搜索中预算分配不佳：探索奖励主导、无望的兄弟节点先于有希望的链扩展、分支与节点质量无关，导致性能受限。

Method: ExTS结合三种机制：判别式奖励塑造以区分狭窄分数分布下的候选；随机虚拟子节点从父节点奖励历史估计新分支价值；质量条件分支仅在节点分数证明预算成本合理时扩展。

Result: 在提示优化、代码生成、分子结构解析和智能体工作流优化中，ExTS与任务特定基线相当或更优，单一固定配置下平均相对增益+5.5%。

Conclusion: ExTS提供了一种有效的预算受限树搜索策略，并引入预运行诊断以理解问题空间差异，为实际适应提供指导。

中文摘要：预算受限的智能体搜索出现在当LLM智能体必须在较小的评估预算下优化候选时，因为验证成本昂贵、生成需要多次模型调用，或两者兼有。在这种机制下，标准MCTS的预算分配不佳：探索奖励在低访问次数时占主导，没有希望的兄弟节点会在有希望的链条加深之前被扩展，且分支与节点质量无关。我们提出了ExTS，一种将扩展本身视为信息价值决策的树搜索策略。ExTS结合了三种机制：判别式奖励塑造（在狭窄的分数分布下区分候选）、随机虚拟子节点（根据父节点的奖励历史估计创建新分支的价值）、以及质量条件分支（仅在节点分数证明预算成本合理时才进行扩展）。在提示优化、代码生成、分子结构解析和智能体工作流优化方面，ExTS与任务特定的树搜索基线相比具有竞争力或有所改进，使用单一固定配置平均相对增益为+5.5%。我们进一步引入了预运行诊断，用于表征预算受限的智能体搜索问题在结构上的相互差异，既提供了对问题空间的理解，也为适应提供了实用指导。

Original abstract: Budget-constrained agentic search arises when an LLM agent must refine candidates under a small evaluation budget, because validation is expensive, generation requires multiple model calls, or both. In this regime, standard MCTS allocates budget poorly: exploration bonuses dominate at low visit counts, unpromising siblings are expanded before promising chains can deepen, and branching is independent of node quality. We introduce ExTS, a tree-search policy that treats expansion itself as a value-of-information decision. ExTS combines three mechanisms: discriminative reward shaping to separate candidates under narrow score distributions, a stochastic virtual child that estimates the value of creating a new branch from the parent's reward history, and quality-conditioned branching that expands only when a node's score justifies the budget cost. Across prompt optimization, code generation, molecular structure elucidation, and agentic workflow optimization, ExTS is competitive with or improves over task-specific tree-search baselines, with an average relative gain of +5.5% using a single fixed configuration. We further introduce pilot-run diagnostics that characterize what makes budget-constrained agentic search problems structurally different from one another, providing both understanding of the problem space and practical guidance for adaptation.

</details>


### [46] [Semantic Overlays: Mitigating Prompt Injection with Annotations Beyond Tokens and Steering Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23873)
*Joshua Penman*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 21 pages, 4 figures, 13 tables. Interactive demo: https://semantic-overlays.vercel.app. Code and released adapters: https://github.com/JoshuaSP/semantic-overlays

Selection reason: 通过语义覆盖层抑制提示注入，对LLM agent的安全控制与不可信上下文防护有直接借鉴。

TL;DR: 提出语义叠加（Semantic Overlays）技术，通过在冻结模型的残差流上添加小型学习适配器，为片段身份提供带外通道，有效防御提示注入攻击，同时保持内容可读性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型仅通过令牌感知输入，难以区分不同来源的片段（如用户输入、工具输出），攻击者可利用提示注入扰乱模型对片段身份的理解，诱导危险行为。现有方法缺乏非文本通道来传递片段身份信息。

Method: 语义叠加：在预填充阶段将小型学习适配器应用于冻结模型的残差流，为指定片段创建无法被令牌复制的带外注释通道。叠加是可训练、可组合的，能编码复杂语义（如编程语言、可执行性），并可选择性应用。

Result: 在提示注入基准中，SEP分离度从24.3%升至96.5%（效用不变），TensorTrust攻击成功率从34.8%降至6.6%，PIArena四个攻击家族均降至0%合规率，标记片段精确复制率达92.5%。

Conclusion: 语义叠加通过非文本通道明确片段身份，能有效缓解提示注入攻击，同时保持底层内容的透明可读性，为增强语言模型安全性提供了新的通用技术。

中文摘要：语言模型看到的一切都是令牌（token）。服务栈知道每个片段是什么——用户输入、工具输出、指令——但模型必须自己跟踪这些信息，而且它可能会丢失线索或感到困惑：文本可以被写成看起来像任何东西。提示注入（prompt injection）正是这种现象的自然利用。通过扰乱模型对片段身份的理解，攻击者可以诱发出不期望甚至危险的行为。在模型的输入中添加一个非文本通道——一种超越文本的交流片段身份的方式——可以缓解这类攻击。因此，我们提出了一种通用的引导技术，称为语义叠加（Semantic Overlays）：在冻结模型的残差流中，于选定的预填充位置应用小型学习适配器。在片段上叠加一层，就创建了一个带外注释通道，该通道无法被令牌复制。与引导向量（steering vectors）不同，语义叠加是可训练的、自适应的，并且可选择性地应用。叠加可以编码复杂的语义，重塑模型对标记片段的感知：当要求模型在一个断言片段处于不同编程语言的叠加下复制一段代码时，模型会忠实地以所断言的编程语言重写该片段。叠加还可以组合，允许透明地读取底层内容，并可以携带复杂的载荷——包括模型会遵循的祈使命令。将片段标记为“不可执行”的叠加可以防御一大类在不可信上下文中添加指令的提示注入攻击。我们在提示注入基准上报告了强劲的结果：SEP分离度从24.3%提升到96.5%，且效用不变（我们的评分规则；我们还修正了已发布评分器中的一个缺陷），TensorTrust攻击成功率从34.8%下降到6.6%，所有四个PIArena攻击家族都降至0%合规率，同时标记片段保持可读（92.5%的精确复制率）。

Original abstract: Everything a language model sees is tokens. The serving stack knows what each span is -- user input, tool output, instructions -- but the model must keep track of that itself, and it can lose track or be confused: text can be written to read like anything. Prompt injection is a natural exploit of this phenomenon. By scrambling the model's understanding of span identity, an attacker can induce unwanted and potentially dangerous actions. Adding a non-textual channel to the model's input -- a way to communicate span identity beyond text -- mitigates this class of attack. We thus introduce a general steering technique called Semantic Overlays: small learned adapters applied at chosen prefill positions to a frozen model's residual stream. Laying an overlay over a span creates an out-of-band annotation channel that cannot be replicated by tokens. Unlike steering vectors, Semantic Overlays are trained, adaptable, and selectively applied. An overlay can encode complex semantics that reshape how the model perceives the marked span: asked to copy a code snippet under an overlay asserting that it is in a different programming language than it is, the model rewrites the snippet, faithfully, in the asserted language. Overlays are also composable, allow for transparent reading of underlying content, and can carry complex payloads -- including imperatives that the model will follow. An overlay which marks a span as "non-executable" defends against the broad class of prompt injections that add instructions in untrusted context. We report strong results on prompt injection benchmarks: SEP separation rises from 24.3% to 96.5% with utility unchanged (our scoring rule; we also correct a defect in the published grader), TensorTrust attack success rate falls from 34.8% to 6.6%, and all four PIArena attack families drop to 0% compliance, all while marked spans stay readable (92.5% exact copy rate).

</details>


### [47] [AI Finds A Way](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23875)
*Aaron Dharna,Cong Lu,Ryan Sullivan,Joel Lehman,Victoria Krakovna,Jeff Clune*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 26 Anecdotes, 40 Pages (59 with references/appendix), updated affiliation footnote

Selection reason: 系统整理AI出现意外行为与奖励黑客的案例，对agent可靠性与对齐风险研究有启示。

TL;DR: 本文收集了26个机器学习轶事，展示AI常以出人意料的方式规避人类设计限制，并讨论这对AI安全与科学发现的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统常表现出非预期和创造性行为，但这些非常规行为在文献中缺乏系统记录；理解这些行为对对齐和安全性至关重要。

Method: 汇集来自多个机器学习子领域、涉及100多位研究者的26个第一手案例，并结合强化学习与基座模型的分析进行归纳。

Result: 说明奖励驱动优化可能因模型利用未充分指定的奖励或约束而失败；互联网规模基座模型并未解决反而可能加剧这些挑战，但同一学习动态也能加速科学发现。

Conclusion: 意外行为在现代AI中普遍存在，研究界需在保持创造力的同时预见和管理不可预测的解决方案，以促进安全且有益的发展。

中文摘要：人工智能（AI）算法经常学习到富有创造性和出乎意料的解决方案，甚至让开发和研究它们的专家研究人员感到惊讶。它们常常令从业者震惊，通过发现未曾预料的行为、利用奖励信号中的漏洞，或自发揭示先前未知的科学现象。然而，在机器学习中，关于这种非常规行为的记述很少被正式记录。这项工作呈现了来自不同机器学习子领域的26个精选第一手轶事，代表了100多名研究者的工作。这些轶事展示了现代AI系统规避人类设定的设计限制并发现我们在训练它们时未预料到的解决方案的能力。此外，这些记述对于未来AI系统的安全性尤为重要。它们说明了在不让模型创造力减退的情况下将模型与人类价值观对齐这一根本挑战，以便它们能够做出惊人发现而不产生令人惊讶的、可能有害的后果。论文首先详细介绍了AI通过强化学习在许多具有挑战性的领域取得超人成功。然而，当模型学会利用未充分指定的奖励或未明确表述的约束时，奖励驱动优化可能会失败。随后我们呈现的案例研究表明，利用互联网规模的基座模型（FMs）并未解决这些根本性挑战，事实上还可能加剧它们。尽管如此，我们认为这些相同的学习动态可以被用来加速科学发现。最后，我们希望这项工作提供一个整合的资源来为未来研究提供信息，并证明倾向于意外行为在现代AI中很常见，突显了预期和管理AI创新但不可预测解决方案能力的必要性。（摘要删节）

Original abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) algorithms frequently learn creative and unexpected solutions, surprising even expert researchers who develop and study them. They often astonish practitioners by discovering unanticipated behavior, exploiting loopholes in reward signals, or spontaneously uncovering previously unknown scientific phenomena. However, accounts of such unconventional behavior across machine learning are seldom formally documented. This work presents 26 curated firsthand anecdotes from various machine learning subfields representing the work of over 100 researchers. These anecdotes showcase the capability of modern AI systems to circumvent human-imposed design limitations and discover unexpected solutions to the tasks we train them on. Furthermore, these accounts are particularly important for the safety of future AI systems. They illustrate the fundamental challenge of aligning models with human values without diminishing their creativity, so they can make surprising discoveries without producing surprising, potentially harmful outcomes. The paper first details AI achieving superhuman success through reinforcement learning across many challenging domains. However, reward-driven optimization can fail when the model learns to hack an underspecified reward or unarticulated constraint. We then present case studies suggesting that harnessing internet-scale foundation models (FMs) has not resolved these fundamental challenges and, in fact, can supercharge them. Nevertheless, we argue that these same learning dynamics can be harnessed to accelerate scientific discovery. Finally, we hope this work provides a consolidated resource to inform future research and demonstrates that the tendency toward unexpected behaviors is commonplace in modern AI, highlighting the need to anticipate and manage AI's capacity for innovative, yet unpredictable, solutions. (abstract abridged)

</details>


### [48] [Quantifying System-Level Harms from AI Adoption in Complex Sociotechnical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23906)
*Paul Vautravers,Oliver Chalkley,Gabriel Downer,Kate S,Damian Ruck*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 从模型行为到系统级风险的量化框架，可迁移到agent可靠性与安全评测。

TL;DR: 提出了一个结合结构化危害分析、组件级测试和概率系统建模的框架，以量化AI在复杂系统中的系统级风险，并通过英国RTGS系统实例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI评估以模型为中心，难以从模型行为推断系统级风险，缺乏对AI失败“那又怎样”问题的回答和系统性影响的量化。

Method: 构建框架，集成STPA危害分析、组件级对抗性实验和概率金融传染模型，并在英国RTGS系统上演示从组件行为到系统结果的映射。

Result: 对抗性输入引起AI建议行为偏移，在系统映射下增加银行失败数量并降低触发级联破坏的冲击阈值，尤其在AI采用广泛或垄断时更为显著。

Conclusion: 该框架为实现基于证据和预期性的AI治理提供了可追踪的量化路径，有助于评估AI在关键系统中的真实影响。

中文摘要：人工智能（AI）正日益融入复杂的社会技术系统，包括关键国家基础设施（CNI），在这些系统中，危害源于技术、人力和组织元素之间的相互作用。然而，当前的AI评估仍以模型为中心，对观察到的行为如何转化为系统级风险提供的洞察很少。我们提出了一个框架，将结构化危害分析、组件级测试和概率系统建模联系起来，以弥合这一差距。通过提供从模型行为到系统级结果的可追溯路径，该框架使从业者能够回答AI失败的“那又怎样”问题，量化其系统性影响，并朝着对复杂系统中AI进行基于证据和预期治理的方向迈进。作为说明性实例，应用于英国的实时全额结算（RTGS）系统，我们使用系统理论过程分析（STPA）推导出AI驱动的损失情景，并检查基于LLM的交易对抗性操纵作为这样一种损失情景。组件级实验表明，简单的对抗性输入会在AI建议被遵循的地方引起可测量的行为转变。在本文使用的组件到系统映射下，对于金融传染模型，这些转变改变了系统弹性，增加了银行倒闭，并降低了冲击导致级联破坏的阈值，特别是在广泛或垄断性AI采用的情况下。

Original abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) is increasingly integrated into complex sociotechnical systems, including Critical National Infrastructure (CNI), where harms emerge from interactions between technical, human, and organisational elements. Yet current AI evaluation remains model-centric, offering little insight into how observed behaviours might translate into system-level risk. We propose a framework that links structured hazard analysis, component-level testing, and probabilistic system modelling to bridge this gap. By providing a traceable pathway from model behaviour to system-level outcomes, the framework enables practitioners to answer the "so what?" of AI failures, quantify their systemic impact, and move toward evidence-based and anticipatory governance of AI in complex systems. Applied to the UK's Real Time Gross Settlement (RTGS) system as an illustrative worked example, we derive AI-driven loss scenarios using Systems Theoretic Process Analysis (STPA) and examine adversarial manipulation of LLM-based trading as one such loss scenario. Component-level experiments show that simple adversarial inputs induce measurable behavioural shifts where AI recommendations are followed. Under the component-to-system mapping used here for a financial contagion model, these shifts alter system resilience, increasing bank failures and lowering the threshold at which shocks lead to cascading disruption, particularly under widespread or monopolistic AI adoption.

</details>


### [49] [Retrieval-augmented generation vs. deterministic tax computation in multi-agent financial advisory: A 2x2 factorial experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23908)
*Aryan Brar,Justin Du,Avery Lor,Kylie Seto,Eric Taylor*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 多agent金融咨询中比较RAG与确定性工具的效果，实证揭示工具增强可能引入冲突信号，对agent工具设计有启发。

TL;DR: 研究发现，为LLM智能体增加税务优化引擎反而显著降低了税收节省，而RAG上下文无显著影响，仅靠模型内化知识效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 税损收割对长期投资有益，但实施复杂且高度依赖具体持仓和个人情况，因此需要探究不同上下文提供机制（如计算引擎和RAG）对多智能体推荐系统建议质量的影响。

Method: 构建了自定义资本利得计算引擎和基于RAG的市场咨询报告向量存储，作为多智能体交易推荐系统的上下文，通过2x2重复测量方差分析比较不同条件（有无引擎、有无RAG）下的相对资本利得和税收节省。

Result: 税务优化引擎主效应显著，启用后税收节省减少约55个百分点；RAG主效应及交互效应不显著；仅RAG条件平均税收节省最高（47.7%），基线次之（30.6%）。

Conclusion: 用特定领域计算引擎增强LLM智能体并不保证性能改善，甚至可能引入冲突信号；LLM内化的金融知识可能已足够，应谨慎设计外部工具集成。

中文摘要：税损收割对长期投资组合增长具有持续益处；然而，高效实施它通常涉及针对该投资组合中的持仓以及拥有它的个人所特有的复杂考量。我们引入了一个自定义资本利得计算引擎和一个RAG检索的市场咨询报告向量存储，为多智能体交易推荐系统提供上下文。我们研究了每个上下文提供者对推荐质量的影响，以投资组合清算期间产生的相对资本利得来衡量。一项2x2重复测量方差分析显示，税务优化引擎的主效应显著（F(1,29)=9.17，p=.005，η²_p=.240）：启用该引擎相对于无引擎条件使税收节省减少了约55个百分点。RAG主效应不显著（p=.841），交互作用也不显著（p=.553）。仅RAG条件实现了最高的描述性平均税收节省（47.7%），基线条件表现第二（30.6%），这表明预训练语言模型内化的金融知识可能足以在没有显式工具的情况下提供称职的税损收割建议。这些结果表明，用特定领域的计算引擎增强LLM智能体并不保证性能提升，并可能引入冲突的优化信号。

Original abstract: Tax-loss harvesting demonstrates consistent benefits to long-term portfolio growth; yet implementing it efficiently often involves complex considerations that are specific to the holdings within that portfolio and the individual who owns it. We introduce a custom capital gains calculation engine and a RAG-retrieved vector store of market advisory reports to provide context for a multi-agent trade recommendation system. We investigate the effects of each context provider on the quality of recommendations, measured by relative capital gains incurred during portfolio liquidation. A 2x2 repeated-measures ANOVA revealed a significant main effect of the tax optimization engine ($F(1,29) = 9.17$, $p = .005$, $η^2_p = .240$): enabling the engine reduced tax savings by approximately 55 percentage points relative to the no-engine conditions. The RAG main effect was not significant ($p = .841$), nor was the interaction ($p = .553$). The RAG-only condition achieved the highest descriptive mean tax savings (47.7%), and the baseline condition performed second-best (30.6%), suggesting that the pre-trained language model's internalized financial knowledge may be sufficient for competent tax-loss harvesting recommendations without explicit tooling. These results indicate that augmenting LLM agents with domain-specific computation engines does not guarantee improved performance and may introduce conflicting optimization signals.

</details>


### [50] [PROOF-Gen: From Optimized Data to Better Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23911)
*Anh Ta,Junjie Zhu,Shahin Shayandeh*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 通过反思优化从失败轨迹恢复训练数据，提升tool-calling agent能力，可迁移到agent自我改进与数据蒸馏。

TL;DR: 提出PROOF-Gen，通过逐场景反思优化从教师失败轨迹中恢复黄金轨迹，显著提升工具调用代理的微调效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练流水线采用生成-过滤机制，失败轨迹被丢弃，导致困难场景反复失败；τ2-bench上57%的教师试验失败，其中三分之二是仅因一个决定性错误而未通过的近失轨迹，缺乏从中学习的信号。

Method: PROOF-Gen为每个失败任务使用反思器分析执行轨迹和评估反馈，生成纠正性指导以引导教师产生通过轨迹，训练前剥离指导，使学生仅从干净演示中学习。

Result: 在τ2-bench上恢复93%失败场景；Qwen3-4B的Pass^1从0.132升至0.529，Gemma 4 E4B在BFCL v4多轮提升+7.2pp；部署流水线中目标完成率提升+6.3pp，端侧迁移提升+1.5pp目标完成率及+1.7至+5.0pp响应质量，非英语平均+1.48pp。

Conclusion: 利用失败轨迹的反思优化能有效提升工具调用智能体性能，且迁移到部署场景，证明该方法可行且有实际价值。

中文摘要：基于教师生成轨迹的监督微调是将工具调用能力蒸馏到可部署模型的标准第一阶段。驱动已上线工具调用智能体的后训练流水线会以每日或每周的节奏重新运行该阶段，每个周期都需支付前沿教师模型的成本，然而其机制是“生成加过滤”（保留教师通过的轨迹，丢弃其余部分），并且每个周期都会留下相同的困难场景，因为失败不提供任何信号。在τ2-bench上，57%的教师试验失败，其中三分之二是“差一点”（大多数工具调用正确，却被一个决定性错误破坏）。我们提出了PROOF-Gen（逐场景反思优化以克服失败生成），通过逐场景提示优化从这些失败中恢复黄金轨迹。对于每个失败任务，反思器分析执行轨迹和评估反馈，然后编写纠正性指导，引导教师生成通过的轨迹。该指导在训练前被剥离，因此学生从干净的演示中学习，无需任务特定的脚手架。在τ2-bench上，逐场景优化恢复了93%的失败场景。在合并数据上微调后，Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507的Pass^1从0.132提升到0.529，Gemma 4 E4B-it在BFCL v4多轮上获得+7.2个百分点的提升。在已部署的流水线中，该方法将轨迹质量提升+6.3个百分点的目标完成率，并迁移到已部署的端侧模型（目标完成率+1.5个百分点；响应质量指标+1.7至+5.0个百分点），且在每种语言环境中均呈现正向迁移（非英语平均+1.48个百分点）。

Original abstract: Supervised fine-tuning on teacher-generated trajectories is the standard first stage for distilling tool-calling capabilities into deployable models. Post-training pipelines that drive shipped tool-calling agents re-run this stage on a daily or weekly cadence, paying the frontier-teacher cost each cycle, yet the mechanism is generate-and-filter (keep the teacher's passing trajectories, discard the rest) and each cycle leaves behind the same hard scenarios because failures supply no signal. On τ2-bench, 57% of teacher trials fail, two-thirds of them near-misses (most tool calls correct, undone by one decisive error).
  We introduce PROOF-Gen (Per-scenario Reflective Optimization to Overcome FailedGeneration), which recovers golden trajectories from these failures via per-scenario prompt optimization. For each failed task, a reflector analyzes the execution trace and evaluation feedback, then writes corrective guidance that steers the teacher to a passing trajectory. The guidance is stripped before training, so the student learns from clean demonstrations with no task-specific scaffold.
  On τ2-bench, per-scenario optimization recovers 93% of failed scenarios. Fine-tuned on the combined data, Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 improves from Pass^1=0.132 to 0.529 and Gemma 4 E4B-it gains +7.2pp on BFCL v4 multi-turn. In a deployed pipeline, the method lifts trajectory quality by +6.3pp goal completion and transfers to a deployed on-device model (+1.5pp goal completion; +1.7 to +5.0pp across response-quality metrics), with positive transfer in every locale (non-English average +1.48pp).

</details>


### [51] [MARS: Multi-Specialist LLM Relay System for Competitive Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23918)
*Andrei Mikhailov,Mikhail Burtsev,Alsu Sagirova*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 13 pages, 8 figures, EMNLP 2026

Selection reason: 多专家LLM协作解决编程竞赛问题，结合检索与沙箱验证，与SemOpt方向直接相关。

TL;DR: 提出了MARS框架，通过专业多智能体中继和检索增强生成，显著提升了大语言模型在竞争性编程中的代码生成准确率，同时降低了成本和方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理竞争性编程问题时，多智能体分工过于通用，算法技术选择完全依赖骨干模型，导致持续失败。因此需要一种方法让多个专业LLM协作，通过检索相关算法知识来提升代码生成质量。

Method: MARS是一个仅提示的框架，每个智能体是算法主题专家（如动态规划、图、字符串、几何），通过检索增强生成基于算法理论语料库。给定问题，检索选择相关专家团队；起始者生成初始C++17解决方案，后续每轮在沙箱中运行公开示例，当前专家可保留、修复或移交草稿，并将结构化数据包传给下一个专家。最后通过基础设施修复规范化样板代码。

Result: 在CodeContests测试集上，MARS达到0.624±0.006通过率，每个任务2.3个流水线阶段，比直接提示高14.4个百分点，以3.3倍更低墙钟成本接近CodeSIM的0.731，且令牌消耗方差更小。

Conclusion: MARS展示了通过专业专家和检索增强多智能体中继能有效解决竞争性编程中的算法选择问题，在性能和效率上取得良好平衡，代码已开源。

中文摘要：大型语言模型在代码生成方面表现出色，但竞争性编程暴露了一个持续存在的失败模式：现有的多智能体流水线将工作分配给通用的规划者、编码者和调试者角色，并将算法技术的选择完全委托给骨干模型。我们提出了MARS（专业LLM的多智能体中继），一个仅提示的框架，其中每个智能体都是主题专家——动态规划、图、字符串、几何等——通过检索增强生成基于算法理论语料库。给定一个问题，检索选择一组相关的专家智能体；一个起始者编写初始的C++17解决方案，随后每一轮在沙箱中对候选方案运行公开示例，让当前活动的专家保留、修复或将草稿移交给下一个专家，并将结构化数据包转发给下一个专家。最后进行一次基础设施修复传递，规范化样板代码。在CodeContests测试集上，使用Gemma 4，MARS达到了0.624±0.006的通过率，每个任务记录2.3个流水线阶段（比直接提示提高了+14.4个百分点），以3.3倍更低的墙钟成本缩小了与CodeSIM（0.731）的大部分差距，并且在每个任务的令牌消耗上方差显著更小。源代码可在GitHub上获取：https://github.com/fckand/mars。

Original abstract: Large Language Models excel at code generation, yet competitive programming exposes a persistent failure mode: existing multi-agent pipelines distribute work over generic planner, coder, and debugger roles and delegate the choice of algorithmic technique to the backbone alone. We present MARS (Multi-Agent Relay of Specialized LLMs), a prompt-only framework in which each agent is a topic specialist---dynamic programming, graphs, strings, geometry, and so on---grounded by retrieval-augmented generation over an algorithm-theory corpus. Given a problem, retrieval selects a small team of relevant specialists; a starter writes an initial C++17 solution, and each subsequent turn runs the candidate against public examples in a sandbox, lets the active specialist keep, repair, or hand off the draft, and forwards a structured packet to the next specialist. A single infrastructure-fixer pass normalizes boilerplate at the end. On the CodeContests test split with Gemma 4, MARS reaches $0.624 \pm 0.006$ pass rate at $2.3$ recorded pipeline stages per task ($+14.4$ percentage points over direct prompting), closing most of the gap to CodeSIM ($0.731$) at $3.3{\times}$ lower wall-clock cost and substantially smaller variance in per-task token spend. The source code is available on GitHub: https://github.com/fckand/mars.

</details>


### [52] [More Rejective, Not More Discriminative: The Unit of Verification in Pre-Execution LLM Oversight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23941)
*Yuchen Han,Cheng Yan,Wuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 37 pages, 20 figures, 32 tables, including appendices

Selection reason: 研究预执行监督中验证单位的选择，提出twin-prefix框架，对agent动作审查与可靠性评测有重要方法贡献。

TL;DR: 提出孪生前缀框架，首次受控测量审查单位长度对LLM监控器的影响，发现短单位（1-2个行动）的informedness最优，长窗口增加拒绝而非区分度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI控制协议中，预执行监控的审查单位（每次调用审查的行动数）被当作给定，其影响未被量化；自然轨迹无法隔离单位效应，且仅捕获率有误导性，需要受控实验来指导安全案例设计。

Method: 开发孪生前缀框架：每个黄金计划生成一个注入错误的前缀和一个仅一个写入不同的清洁孪生，在五个嵌套长度上成对评判；使用预注册的informedness（捕获率-误拒率）作为区分度指标，并通过对保留观察的重放分析失败原因。

Result: 更长的审查提高捕获率但同步增加误拒率；在所有六个评判者和两个领域中，informedness在1-2个行动时达到峰值；失败主要由观察剥夺导致；校准的短单位在八行动审查中恢复高达0.95的informedness，且无标签盲策略能一致超越。

Conclusion: 安全案例应明确审查单位并同时报告清洁序列；短单位是更优选择，框架是首个受控、预注册的验证工具，可避免单独使用捕获率的陷阱。

中文摘要：预执行监督是人工智能控制中可信监控的核心：一个可能出错的 LLM 监控器在不可逆执行之前审查计划中的行动。过度拦截会丧失实用性，并迫使部署者将其禁用。每个协议都必须确定一个验证单位：一次调用审查多少个行动。现有设计将单位视为给定；其对可能出错的监控器的影响未被测量。自然轨迹无法将其隔离：审查长度与错误类型和位置共同变化。仅凭捕获率会误导：拒绝一切能捕获一切。测量这一点需要仅边界变化和一个匹配的清洁对照。我们引入了“孪生前缀”框架，它同时提供了这两者。每个黄金计划生成一个带有一个人为注入且环境接受的错误的前缀，以及一个仅在一个写入上不同的清洁孪生。在五个嵌套长度上评判每一对，将判决变化仅归因于单位。区分度通过预注册的 informedness（捕获率减去误拒率）评分。更长的审查提高捕获率；误拒率同步上升。对于两个领域中的所有六位评判者，informedness 在一个或两个行动时达到峰值：更长的窗口使零样本监控器更倾向于拒绝，而非更具区分度。回放被保留的观察结果将失败主要归因于观察剥夺。安全案例应说明单位并同时报告清洁序列。我们的框架是首个针对此选择的受控、预注册工具，且从不单独读取捕获率。我们校准的短单位在八行动审查中恢复了高达 0.95 的 informedness，并且没有测试的标签盲策略能一致地击败它。

Original abstract: Pre-execution oversight is core to trusted monitoring in AI control: a fallible LLM monitor vets planned actions before irreversible execution. Over-blocking forfeits usefulness and pressures deployers to disable it. Every protocol must fix a unit of verification: how many actions one call reviews. Existing designs take the unit as given; its effect on fallible monitors is unmeasured. Natural traces cannot isolate it: review length co-varies with error type and position. Catch alone misleads: rejecting everything catches everything. Measuring this needs boundary variation alone and a matched clean control. We introduce the twin-prefix framework, which supplies both. Each gold plan yields a prefix with one injected, environment-accepted error and a clean twin differing in one write. Judging each pair at five nested lengths ties verdict changes to the unit alone. Discrimination is scored by pre-registered informedness, catch minus false rejection. Longer review raises catch; false rejection climbs in lockstep. Informedness peaks at one or two actions for all six judges in both domains: longer windows make zero-shot monitors more rejective, not more discriminative. Replaying withheld observations traces the failure largely to observation deprivation. Safety cases should state the unit and co-report the clean series. Our framework is the first controlled, pre-registered instrument for this choice and never reads catch alone. Our calibrated short unit recovers up to 0.95 informedness over eight-action review, and no tested label-blind policy consistently beats it.

</details>


### [53] [Recursive Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23956)
*Shengxin Zhang,Xiaomin Wu,Xiyang Wu,Jing Xie*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 统一递归推理运算符并大规模实证比较分支、深化、分解策略，对agent测试时推理设计与评估有直接价值。

TL;DR: 提出统一视角将测试时推理方法视为递归算子（GROW、PRUNE、BRANCH），在共享框架下比较，发现BRANCH在所有设置中一致最优，平均提升5.98个百分点，且其优势与从截断中恢复相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时推理方法（如迭代细化、分解、重复采样）通常被孤立评估，导致跨模型和基准的收益难以比较。需要统一框架来公平比较这些方法。

Method: 引入三种递归算子：GROW（加深路径）、PRUNE（分解重组）、BRANCH（采样并选择），在相同提示、token预算和评分代码下与单次思维链基线对比，并进行配对分析。

Result: BRANCH在14个设置中全部提升，平均+5.98个百分点，12个设置最优；GROW平均+2.18但有两个设置下降；PRUNE平均+0.94。BRANCH的收益与空输出率相关（r=0.72）。

Conclusion: 重复分支在测试时推理中一致占优，无需按问题路由不同算子；同时强调配对评分协议的重要性，以避免评估流程导致的错误结论。

中文摘要：测试时推理方法，如迭代细化、分解和重复采样，通常被孤立地评估，使得它们的收益难以跨模型、基准和评估流程进行比较。我们引入了一种统一的视角，将这些方法视为智能体推理轨迹上的递归算子：GROW，加深单一推理路径；PRUNE，分解并重组问题；以及BRANCH，采样替代推理路径并在其中进行选择。我们在共享测试框架下，使用相同的提示、token预算和评分代码，将这三个算子与单次思维链基线进行了评估。在五个基准和三个前沿模型上，共涵盖14个模型-基准设置、49,327个评分项目和151,876次模型调用，BRANCH在所有14个设置中平均提高了5.98个百分点，并且在12个设置中是最优算子。相比之下，GROW平均提升2.18个百分点，但在两个设置中性能下降；PRUNE平均提升0.94个百分点。分析表明，BRANCH的优势不仅来自于探索多条推理路径，还来自于从截断中恢复：其收益与基线中空输出、预算耗尽的比率强相关（r = 0.72）。这些结果削弱了不同问题需要在测试时推理算子之间进行路由的假设；在这种抽象层次上，重复分支始终占主导地位。最后，我们表明，非配对评估以及将评分流程失败视为模型错误可能会实质性改变甚至反转比较结论，这促使配对评分作为测试时计算评估的标准协议。
